oṃ hrauṃ alakṣmīrme naśyatāṃ—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ hraḥ tvāṃ vṛṇe—karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ*) ओं ह्रां चंद्रां प्रभासां—हृदयाय नमः । ओं ह्रीं यशसाज्वलंतीं—शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं हूं श्रियं लोके देवजुष्टामुदारां—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं हैं तां पद्मनीमीं शरणमहं प्रपद्ये-कवचाय हुं । ओं ह्रौं अलक्ष्मीर्मे नश्यतांनेत्रत्रयाय वौषट् । ओं ह्रः त्वां वृणे—अस्त्राय फट् । भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ ध्यानं ॥ तेजोमंडलमध्यगां द्विनयनां दिव्यांबरालंकृतां देवीं पद्मवराभयां करतलैश्चिंतामणिं बिभ्रतीं । नानादिव्यविभूषणां च भजतां दौर्भाग्यसंहारिणीं नानाभीष्टवरप्रदाननिरतां वंदे परां देवतां । (*oṃ hrāṃ caṃdrāṃ prabhāsāṃ—hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ yaśasājvalaṃtīṃ—śirase svāhā | oṃ hūṃ śriyaṃ loke devajuṣṭāmudārāṃ—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ haiṃ tāṃ padmanīmīṃ śaraṇamahaṃ prapadye-kavacāya huṃ | oṃ hrauṃ alakṣmīrme naśyatāṃnetratrayāya vauṣaṭ | oṃ hraḥ tvāṃ vṛṇe—astrāya phaṭ | bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ ‖ tejomaṃḍalamadhyagāṃ dvinayanāṃ divyāṃbarālaṃkṛtāṃ devīṃ padmavarābhayāṃ karatalaiściṃtāmaṇiṃ bibhratīṃ | nānādivyavibhūṣaṇāṃ ca bhajatāṃ daurbhāgyasaṃhāriṇīṃ nānābhīṣṭavarapradānaniratāṃ vaṃde parāṃ devatāṃ |*)

### Printed p. 291

चंद्रां प्रभासां यशसा ज्वलंतीं श्रियं लोके देवजुष्टामुदारां । तां पद्मनीमां शरणमहं प्रपद्ये अलक्ष्मीर्मे नश्यतां त्वां वृणे ॥ (*caṃdrāṃ prabhāsāṃ yaśasā jvalaṃtīṃ śriyaṃ loke devajuṣṭāmudārāṃ | tāṃ padmanīmāṃ śaraṇamahaṃ prapadye alakṣmīrme naśyatāṃ tvāṃ vṛṇe ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

आत्राहंशब्दप्रक्षेपः प्रामादिकः । चंद्रां चंद्रवत् प्रकाशमानां प्रभासां प्रकृष्टा भाः कांतियस्यास्त्यां । आपं चेत्याप् । यशसा कीर्त्या ज्वलंतीं प्रकाशमानां । देवजुष्टां देवैरिंद्राद्यैस्सेवितां प्रीतां वा । उदारां प्रगल्भां वा पद्मिनीं पद्मलतारूपां पद्माकारां वा । ईमितिनिपातः अनर्थकः— “मिताक्षरेष्वनर्थकः किमीमिद्विति” यास्कवचनात् । (*ātrāhaṃśabdaprakṣepaḥ prāmādikaḥ | caṃdrāṃ caṃdravat prakāśamānāṃ prabhāsāṃ prakṛṣṭā bhāḥ kāṃtiyasyāstyāṃ | āpaṃ cetyāp | yaśasā kīrtyā jvalaṃtīṃ prakāśamānāṃ | devajuṣṭāṃ devairiṃdrādyaissevitāṃ prītāṃ vā | udārāṃ pragalbhāṃ vā padminīṃ padmalatārūpāṃ padmākārāṃ vā | īmitinipātaḥ anarthakaḥ— “mitākṣareṣvanarthakaḥ kimīmidviti” yāskavacanāt |*) ईकारवाच्यां वा तां श्रियं लोके इहलोके शरणं रक्षित्रीं प्रपद्ये प्रपन्मोऽस्मि । अहमिति शेषः । अतः हे श्रीः मे मम अलक्ष्मीः आश्रीः नश्यतां नाशं प्राप्नोतु । त्वां वृणे शरणत्वेन कुर्वॆ ॥ (*īkāravācyāṃ vā tāṃ śriyaṃ loke ihaloke śaraṇaṃ rakṣitrīṃ prapadye prapanmo 'smi | ahamiti śeṣaḥ | ataḥ he śrīḥ me mama alakṣmīḥ āśrīḥ naśyatāṃ nāśaṃ prāpnotu | tvāṃ vṛṇe śaraṇatvena kurvè ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The insertion of the word अहं (*ahaṃ*) here is an error of the copyists. चंद्रां (*caṃdrāṃ*) is one shining like the moon; प्रभासां (*prabhāsāṃ*) is she who has excellent radiance (the feminine affix being by the rule आपं चेत्याप् (*āpaṃ cetyāp*)). यशसा ज्वलंतीं (*yaśasā jvalaṃtīṃ*) is one shining with fame; देवजुष्टां (*devajuṣṭāṃ*) is one served by gods such as Indra, or pleasing to them; उदारां (*udārāṃ*) is bold or generous; पद्मिनीं (*padminīṃ*) is of the form of a lotus-creeper or of a lotus. The particle ईं (*īṃ*) is without meaning, on the authority of Yāska's statement मिताक्षरेष्वनर्थकः किमीमिद्विति (*mitākṣareṣvanarthakaḥ kimīmidviti*); or else it denotes the syllable ī (Śrī). That Śrī, the protectress in this world, I take as my refuge. Therefore, O Śrī, may my misfortune be destroyed; I choose you as my refuge.

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdharācārya**

चंद्रां संतोषकारिणीं प्रभासां प्रकर्षण भास्वतीं । लोके लोकेषु चतुर्दशसु । यशसा ज्वलंतीमैश्वर्यसंपदौदार्यादिप्रयुक्तकीर्त्या प्रकाशमानां । आत एव देवजुष्टामिंद्रादिसेवितां । उदारां विस्तारां सर्वव्यापिनीमित्यर्थः । पद्मिनीं करधृतकमलामिति यावत् । ईं ईकारवाच्यां तादृशीं श्रियमहं शरणं प्रपद्ये रक्षकी भवेति व्रजामि । मे मम अलक्ष्मीर्या सा नश्यतां नाशं प्राप्नोतु । त्वां लक्ष्मीमहं वृणे मदाश्रितज्येष्ठानिस्सारणाय त्वां प्रति वरणं करिष्ये । (*caṃdrāṃ saṃtoṣakāriṇīṃ prabhāsāṃ prakarṣaṇa bhāsvatīṃ | loke lokeṣu caturdaśasu | yaśasā jvalaṃtīmaiśvaryasaṃpadaudāryādiprayuktakīrtyā prakāśamānāṃ | āta eva devajuṣṭāmiṃdrādisevitāṃ | udārāṃ vistārāṃ sarvavyāpinīmityarthaḥ | padminīṃ karadhṛtakamalāmiti yāvat | īṃ īkāravācyāṃ tādṛśīṃ śriyamahaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapadye rakṣakī bhaveti vrajāmi | me mama alakṣmīryā sā naśyatāṃ nāśaṃ prāpnotu | tvāṃ lakṣmīmahaṃ vṛṇe madāśritajyeṣṭhānissāraṇāya tvāṃ prati varaṇaṃ kariṣye |*) नात्राहंशब्दः प्रक्षिप्तः—“सार्वदेशिकशिष्टपाठसंप्रतिपत्ते” रित्यात्तरीयाः । दाक्षिणात्यपाठे तददर्शनात्पृक्षिप्त एव ॥ (*nātrāhaṃśabdaḥ prakṣiptaḥ—“sārvadeśikaśiṣṭapāṭhasaṃpratipatte” rityāttarīyāḥ | dākṣiṇātyapāṭhe tadadarśanātpṛkṣipta eva ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

चंद्रां (*caṃdrāṃ*) is she who gives delight; प्रभासां (*prabhāsāṃ*) is greatly shining; लोके (*loke*) means in the fourteen worlds; यशसा ज्वलंतीं (*yaśasā jvalaṃtīṃ*) is one shining with fame arising from lordship, wealth, generosity and the like; therefore देवजुष्टां (*devajuṣṭāṃ*) is served by Indra and the others; उदारां (*udārāṃ*) is extended, all-pervading; पद्मिनीं (*padminīṃ*) is one holding a lotus in her hand; ईं (*īṃ*) denotes the syllable ī, so such a Śrī I approach as refuge, that she may become my protector. May whatever misfortune is mine be destroyed; I choose you, Lakṣmī, in order to drive out the misfortune that clings to me. Some say the word अहं (*ahaṃ*) has not been inserted here, on the authority of the agreement of the texts of all regions; since it is not seen in the southern reading it was interpolated.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- चंद्रां (*caṃdrāṃ*)—she who has a lustre like the moon
- प्रभासां (*prabhāsāṃ*)—she who shines with excellent bodily lustre
- यशसा (*yaśasā*)—by fame, that is, by the fame arising from qualities such as lordship, wealth and generosity
- ज्वलंती (*jvalaṃtī*)—she who is famed or shining
- देवजुष्टां (*devajuṣṭāṃ*)—she who is served by gods such as Indra

### Printed p. 292

- उदारां (*udārāṃ*)—she who gives boons and the like abundantly to devotees, or who is widely extended, pervading everywhere
- पद्मिनीं (*padminīṃ*)—she who bears lotuses in her hands, or who has a body swaying like a lotus-creeper, or who has a bodily form as delightful to the eye as a lotus
- तां ईं श्रियं (*tāṃ īṃ śriyaṃ*)—that same famed goddess Śrī
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- शरणं प्रपद्ये (*śaraṇaṃ prapadye*)—I take refuge, that is, I resort to her for protection (by the grace of this goddess)
- मे (*me*)—my
- अलक्ष्मीः (*alakṣmīḥ*)—misfortune, poverty and the like
- नश्यतां (*naśyatāṃ*)—may they be destroyed (O goddess, I)
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- वृणे (*vṛṇe*)—I pray and call, for my protection

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

**Bhāgadheya-bhūṣaṇa**

श्रीसूक्ते प्रथमे वर्गे प्रजपेत्संचमीमृचं । मायाबीजेन विन्यस्य भक्तितो भुवनेश्वरीं ॥ अक्षीणभासां चंद्राख्यां ज्वलंतीं यशसा श्रियं । देवैर्जुष्टामुदारां च पद्मिनीमां भजाम्यहं ॥ त्वां वृणे त्वत्पसादेन ममालक्ष्मीर्विनश्यतु । (*śrīsūkte prathame varge prajapetsaṃcamīmṛcaṃ | māyābījena vinyasya bhaktito bhuvaneśvarīṃ ‖ akṣīṇabhāsāṃ caṃdrākhyāṃ jvalaṃtīṃ yaśasā śriyaṃ | devairjuṣṭāmudārāṃ ca padminīmāṃ bhajāmyahaṃ ‖ tvāṃ vṛṇe tvatpasādena mamālakṣmīrvinaśyatu |*)

प्रत्यहं त्वेकसाहस्रं गोष्ठस्थः प्रजपेद्यचं ॥ अखंडदीपं प्रज्वाल्य गोघृतेन जितेंद्रियः । प्रत्यक्षरत्रिसाहस्रजपान्मुच्यत संकटैः ॥ मंडलैश्च त्रिभिः पूर्णैर्द्रारिद्रान्मुच्यते ध्रुवं । (*pratyahaṃ tvekasāhasraṃ goṣṭhasthaḥ prajapedyacaṃ ‖ akhaṃḍadīpaṃ prajvālya goghṛtena jiteṃdriyaḥ | pratyakṣaratrisāhasrajapānmucyata saṃkaṭaiḥ ‖ maṃḍalaiśca tribhiḥ pūrṇairdrāridrānmucyate dhruvaṃ |*)

दीक्षितश्च तिलैर्हुत्वा विरजामंगदेवतां ॥ आमिक्षाशी धनं प्राप्य भूमिस्थं राजवंदितः । संग्रामे विजयं प्राप्य चांद्रीं गतिमनाप्नुयात् ॥ अहस्सु प्रजपेत्सौरीं दक्षिणोत्तरमार्गगां । तिलैः श्वेतैस्सहाज्येन जुहुयात्पावके स्वके ॥ जपाद्दशांशं संतर्प्य गंधकर्पूरवारिभिः । होमं कृत्वा दशांशं च पूजयेच्च सुवासिनीः ॥ कमलैरर्चयेद्देवीं गोभ्यो घासं प्रदापयेत् । एतत्पुण्यप्रभावेण दरिद्रोऽपि धनी भवेत् ॥ (*dīkṣitaśca tilairhutvā virajāmaṃgadevatāṃ ‖ āmikṣāśī dhanaṃ prāpya bhūmisthaṃ rājavaṃditaḥ | saṃgrāme vijayaṃ prāpya cāṃdrīṃ gatimanāpnuyāt ‖ ahassu prajapetsaurīṃ dakṣiṇottaramārgagāṃ | tilaiḥ śvetaissahājyena juhuyātpāvake svake ‖ japāddaśāṃśaṃ saṃtarpya gaṃdhakarpūravāribhiḥ | homaṃ kṛtvā daśāṃśaṃ ca pūjayecca suvāsinīḥ ‖ kamalairarcayeddevīṃ gobhyo ghāsaṃ pradāpayet | etatpuṇyaprabhāveṇa daridro 'pi dhanī bhavet ‖*)

ह्रीमित्यादि षडंगन्यासः । ध्यानं— आकाशपद्माकरचंद्रबिंबप्लवोल्लसंतीं परिपूर्णकांतिं । पद्मस्थितां पद्मकरां प्रपद्ये लक्ष्मीलक्ष्मीविनिवृत्तयेऽहं ॥ (*hrīmityādi ṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ | dhyānaṃ— ākāśapadmākaracaṃdrabiṃbaplavollasaṃtīṃ paripūrṇakāṃtiṃ | padmasthitāṃ padmakarāṃ prapadye lakṣmīlakṣmīvinivṛttaye 'haṃ ‖*)

### Printed p. 293

##### Anuvāda

This fifth Ṛk of the Śrī-sūkta is to be recited with the nyāsa made together with the māyā-bīja (hrīṃ), meditating with devotion on the goddess Śrī. That goddess shines always with an unfailing radiance. Like the moon, she is distinguished by a cool lustre and is delightful. Toward her devotees she is famed for the glory of generosity and the like, granting boons such as favour, the fulfilment of wishes and the removal of poverty and ill fortune. She is served by all the gods, Indra and the others. Seated on a lotus seat and holding lotuses in her hands, this goddess Śrī shines; "I worship her always, and I pray and call that goddess for the destruction of my misfortune and poverty and for her grace": so one should pray. Sitting in the cow-pen where cows are tied, or in some other pure place, with undistracted mind, one should recite every day one thousand each time. During the recitation one should put cow's ghee in lamps and keep them lit so that they do not go out. If three thousand are recited for each syllable of this Ṛk, one is freed from all troubles. If this Ṛk is recited without a break for three maṇḍalas (a maṇḍala being forty-eight days), poverty is surely removed. Having taken the dīkṣā, that is, having purified oneself and made the resolve, if one makes oblations in the fire with sesame, with this mantra, together with the goddess Virajā, the subordinate deity, one finds the hidden treasure laid in the earth; one is honoured by kings, is victorious in battle, and at the end attains the world of the moon. In the daytime one should recite meditating on the sun, who travels the southern and northern paths, and make oblations in the fire with white sesame and with clarified butter. A libation is to be made with water mixed with sandal and camphor, to the tenth part of the number of recitations. Then oblations are to be made to the tenth part of the number of libations, and married women (suvāsinī) are to be honoured. The goddess Śrī is to be worshipped with lotus flowers, and fodder such as grass is to be given to cows. By doing so, that is, by the power of the merit of this recitation and these oblations, however poor one may be, one becomes rich. At the time of the nyāsa of the mantra one makes the nyāsa together with the seed-syllable hrīṃ and meditates as in the verse given below.—माडबेकु.—आकाशपद्माकरचंद्रबिंबप्लवोल्लसंतीं परिपूर्णकांतिं । पद्मस्थितां पद्मकरां प्रपद्ये लक्ष्मीमलक्ष्मीविनिवृत्तयेऽहं ॥ (*māḍabeku.—ākāśapadmākaracaṃdrabiṃbaplavollasaṃtīṃ paripūrṇakāṃtiṃ | padmasthitāṃ padmakarāṃ prapadye lakṣmīmalakṣmīvinivṛttaye 'haṃ ‖*)

Here ends the explanation of the fifth Ṛk.

### Printed p. 294

#### Ṛk 6

अस्य श्रीश्रीसूक्तस्य आदित्यवर्णेति षष्ठयज्ञहामंत्रस्य । आनंद ऋषिः । श्रीमहालक्ष्मीर्देवता । त्रिष्टुप्‌छंदः । आं बीजं । श्रीं शक्तिः । ह्रीं कीलकं । सर्वार्थसिद्धये महालक्ष्मीप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ओं आदित्यवर्णे तपसोऽधिजातः—अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं वनस्पतिस्तव— तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं वृक्षोऽथ बिल्वः—मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं तस्य फलानि तपसा नुदंतु—अनामिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं मायांतरायाश्च—कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं बाह्या अलक्ष्मीः—करतलकरपृष्ठाभ्यां नमः ॥ ओं आदित्यवर्णे तपसोऽधिजातः— हृदयाय नमः । ओं वनस्पतिस्तव—शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं वृक्षोऽथ बिल्वः—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं तस्य फलानि तपसा नुदंतु—कवचाय हुं । ओं मायांतरायाश्च— नेत्रत्रयाय नौषट् । ओं बाह्या अलक्ष्मीः—आस्त्राय फट् ॥ भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ (*asya śrīśrīsūktasya ādityavarṇeti ṣaṣṭhayajñahāmaṃtrasya | ānaṃda ṛṣiḥ | śrīmahālakṣmīrdevatā | triṣṭupchaṃdaḥ | āṃ bījaṃ | śrīṃ śaktiḥ | hrīṃ kīlakaṃ | sarvārthasiddhaye mahālakṣmīprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ oṃ ādityavarṇe tapaso 'dhijātaḥ—aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ vanaspatistava— tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ vṛkṣo 'tha bilvaḥ—madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ tasya phalāni tapasā nudaṃtu—anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ māyāṃtarāyāśca—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ bāhyā alakṣmīḥ—karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ ‖ oṃ ādityavarṇe tapaso 'dhijātaḥ— hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ vanaspatistava—śirase svāhā | oṃ vṛkṣo 'tha bilvaḥ—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ tasya phalāni tapasā nudaṃtu—kavacāya huṃ | oṃ māyāṃtarāyāśca— netratrayāya nauṣaṭ | oṃ bāhyā alakṣmīḥ—āstrāya phaṭ ‖ bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖*) अथ ध्यानं ॥ श्रीमन्मंटपमध्यचक्रविलसत्तार्क्ष्यां ससंस्थस्य मे देवीं दक्षिणवक्षसि प्रणयिनीं पद्मौवरौ बिभ्रतीं। कोटीरांगदहारनूपुरलसत्ताटंकनासामणिं कस्तूरीतिलकांचितां शशिमुखीं वंदे परां देवतां ॥ उद्यदादित्यसंकाशां बिल्वकाननमध्यगां । तनुमध्यां श्रियं ध्यायेदलक्ष्मीपरिहारिणीं ॥ इति ध्यात्वा जपं कुर्यात् ॥ (*atha dhyānaṃ ‖ śrīmanmaṃṭapamadhyacakravilasattārkṣyāṃ sasaṃsthasya me devīṃ dakṣiṇavakṣasi praṇayinīṃ padmauvarau bibhratīṃ| koṭīrāṃgadahāranūpuralasattāṭaṃkanāsāmaṇiṃ kastūrītilakāṃcitāṃ śaśimukhīṃ vaṃde parāṃ devatāṃ ‖ udyadādityasaṃkāśāṃ bilvakānanamadhyagāṃ | tanumadhyāṃ śriyaṃ dhyāyedalakṣmīparihāriṇīṃ ‖ iti dhyātvā japaṃ kuryāt ‖*)

आदित्यवर्णे तपसोऽधिजातो वनस्पतिस्तव वृक्षोऽथ बिल्वः । तस्य फलानि तपसानुदंतु मायांतरायाश्च बाह्या अलक्ष्मीः॥ (*ādityavarṇe tapaso 'dhijāto vanaspatistava vṛkṣo 'tha bilvaḥ | tasya phalāni tapasānudaṃtu māyāṃtarāyāśca bāhyā alakṣmīḥ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

वृक्षोधेत्यथपदमपि बाहुलकात् । आदित्यवर्णे आदित्यस्य सूर्यस्य वर्ण इव वर्णो यस्यास्तस्यास्संबोधनं । हे श्रीः तव तपसः नियमाद्भेतोः वनस्पतिः “अपुष्पाः फलवंतो ये ते वनस्पतयः स्मृताः ” इति मानवात्! बिल्वः बिल्वनामकः वृक्षः द्रुमः अधिजातः प्रादुर्भूतः त्वक्करादिति शेषः । “बिल्वो लक्ष्माः करेऽभवदिति” नामनपुराणे कात्यायनवचनात् । (*vṛkṣodhetyathapadamapi bāhulakāt | ādityavarṇe ādityasya sūryasya varṇa iva varṇo yasyāstasyāssaṃbodhanaṃ | he śrīḥ tava tapasaḥ niyamādbhetoḥ vanaspatiḥ “apuṣpāḥ phalavaṃto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ ” iti mānavāt! bilvaḥ bilvanāmakaḥ vṛkṣaḥ drumaḥ adhijātaḥ prādurbhūtaḥ tvakkarāditi śeṣaḥ | “bilvo lakṣmāḥ kare 'bhavaditi” nāmanapurāṇe kātyāyanavacanāt |*) अथ अनंतरं तस्य बिल्वस्य फलानि पक्वशलाटुकानि तपसा त्वदनुग्रहेणेत्यर्थः । अंतरा अंतरिंद्रियसंबंधिनीः माया अज्ञानं तत्कार्याणि च । बाह्याः बहिरिंद्रियसंबंधिन्यः यास्ताः अलक्ष्मः आश्रियः नुदन्नु आपनुवस्तु निवारयंत्वित्यर्थः ॥ (*atha anaṃtaraṃ tasya bilvasya phalāni pakvaśalāṭukāni tapasā tvadanugraheṇetyarthaḥ | aṃtarā aṃtariṃdriyasaṃbaṃdhinīḥ māyā ajñānaṃ tatkāryāṇi ca | bāhyāḥ bahiriṃdriyasaṃbaṃdhinyaḥ yāstāḥ alakṣmaḥ āśriyaḥ nudannu āpanuvastu nivārayaṃtvityarthaḥ ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The word वृक्षः (*vṛkṣaḥ*) with अथ (*atha*) is also so read, by the Vedic latitude. आदित्यवर्णे (*ādityavarṇe*) is the vocative of her whose colour is like the colour of the sun. O Śrī, by reason of your austerity (niyama) the वनस्पतिः (*vanaspatiḥ*) (as the Mānava text defines, trees that bear fruit without flowers are called vanaspati), the tree named bilva, arose from your hand, as is said in the Nāmana-purāṇa by Kātyāyana: "the bilva arose from Lakṣmī's hand." Then the ripe fruits of that bilva, by your grace, अंतरा (*aṃtarā*) the māyā (ignorance and its effects) connected with the inner senses, and the बाह्याः (*bāhyāḥ*) misfortunes connected with the outer senses, may they drive away and remove.

### Printed p. 295

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdharācārya**

अथेत्यनंतरार्थः । हे आदित्यवर्णे सूर्यसमानकांते तपसः तपश्चर्यार्थं वनस्पतिः यो बिल्ववृक्षः तव निवासयोग्यः अधिजातः उत्पन्नः “ बिल्वाटव्यां महालक्ष्मीरूपास्ते बिल्वनायकमिति ” भार्गवपुराणात् । तस्य त्वत्करजातस्य बिल्ववृक्षस्य “ तनुमध्या स्वहस्तात्थं बिल्वद्रुं समुपाश्रितेति ” भार्गवतंत्रोक्तेश्व । प्रसूनादीनि फलानि मा मां प्रति अंतराः अंतर्गताः अलक्ष्मीः या बाह्याश्च अलक्ष्मीः ताः तव तव तपसा करणेन मदाश्रितान्तरगतबाह्यालक्ष्मीरूपदुर्गतीस्सर्वान् नुदंतित्याशयः ॥ (*athetyanaṃtarārthaḥ | he ādityavarṇe sūryasamānakāṃte tapasaḥ tapaścaryārthaṃ vanaspatiḥ yo bilvavṛkṣaḥ tava nivāsayogyaḥ adhijātaḥ utpannaḥ “ bilvāṭavyāṃ mahālakṣmīrūpāste bilvanāyakamiti ” bhārgavapurāṇāt | tasya tvatkarajātasya bilvavṛkṣasya “ tanumadhyā svahastātthaṃ bilvadruṃ samupāśriteti ” bhārgavataṃtrokteśva | prasūnādīni phalāni mā māṃ prati aṃtarāḥ aṃtargatāḥ alakṣmīḥ yā bāhyāśca alakṣmīḥ tāḥ tava tava tapasā karaṇena madāśritāntaragatabāhyālakṣmīrūpadurgatīssarvān nudaṃtityāśayaḥ ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

अथ (*atha*) has the sense "afterwards." O sun-coloured one, for the sake of your austerity the bilva tree arose, suited to your dwelling, as is said in the Bhārgava-purāṇa and in the Bhārgava-tantra. May the fruits of that tree, born of your hand, by your austerity remove all the misfortunes, inner and outer, that cling to me, the evil states in the form of misfortune: such is the intention.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आदित्यवर्णे (*ādityavarṇe*)—O goddess Śrī, whose bodily lustre shines brilliantly like the radiance of the sun
- तव (*tava*)—your
- तपसा (तपसः) (*tapasā (tapasaḥ)*)—by restraint, or by grace
- वनस्पतिः (*vanaspatiḥ*)—the tree that bears fruit without flowers
- बिल्वः वृक्षः (*bilvaḥ vṛkṣaḥ*)—the tree called bilva
- अधिजातः (*adhijātaḥ*)—arose
- अथ (*atha*)—afterwards
- तस्य (*tasya*)—of that bilva tree
- फलानि (*phalāni*)—the fruits
- तपसा (*tapasā*)—by your grace
- याः (*yāḥ*)—which
- अंतरा (*aṃtarā*)—connected with the inner senses
- माया (*māyā*)—ignorance and its evil results
- बाह्याः च (*bāhyāḥ ca*)—and connected with the outer senses
- आलक्ष्मीः (*ālakṣmīḥ*)—sorrow, poverty, misfortune and the like
- याः (ताः) (*yāḥ (tāḥ)*)—whichever there are, those
- नुदंतु (*nudaṃtu*)—may they destroy (may they remove)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O goddess Śrī, whose body shines with great radiance like the radiance of the sun, by your command (grace) the tree called bilva, which bears fruit only, without letting flowers appear, came into being. Then may the fruits of that bilva tree remove the ignorance lodged in the inner senses of human beings and the sorrow and poverty connected with the outer senses.

**Sārasvatasiddhā**

आदित्यवर्णे तरुणार्कवदरुणवर्णे । " अरुणां करुणातरंगिताक्षीमिति " बालारूपमत्र सूचितं भवति । वनस्पतिबिल्वो वृक्षः तव तपसोऽधिजातः । नीवातटीनीतटी बिल्वारण्यं त्वत्तपसो जातमिति भावः । (*ādityavarṇe taruṇārkavadaruṇavarṇe | " aruṇāṃ karuṇātaraṃgitākṣīmiti " bālārūpamatra sūcitaṃ bhavati | vanaspatibilvo vṛkṣaḥ tava tapaso 'dhijātaḥ | nīvātaṭīnītaṭī bilvāraṇyaṃ tvattapaso jātamiti bhāvaḥ |*) तनुमध्या पुरा बाला नीवातटमुपाश्रिता । बिल्वारण्ये तपश्चक्रे लक्ष्मीर्लोकहितार्थिनी ॥ (*tanumadhyā purā bālā nīvātaṭamupāśritā | bilvāraṇye tapaścakre lakṣmīrlokahitārthinī ‖*)

### Printed p. 296

इति कालिकापुराणवचनात् । तस्य बिल्वस्य फलानि त्वत्तपसा सशक्तिकानि मा मयि स्थिताः अंतराः हृद्गताज्ञानपापादिरूपिण्यः बाह्याः दारिद्राद्रिरूपिण्यः याः दुर्गतयः शाः अलक्ष्मीः नुदंतु निरासयंतु । (*iti kālikāpurāṇavacanāt | tasya bilvasya phalāni tvattapasā saśaktikāni mā mayi sthitāḥ aṃtarāḥ hṛdgatājñānapāpādirūpiṇyaḥ bāhyāḥ dāridrādrirūpiṇyaḥ yāḥ durgatayaḥ śāḥ alakṣmīḥ nudaṃtu nirāsayaṃtu |*)

बिल्वनाथप्रियायास्तु नारायण्यास्तपोबलात् । बिल्वारण्यं फलत्यद्य लोकालक्ष्मीनिवृत्तये ॥ (*bilvanāthapriyāyāstu nārāyaṇyāstapobalāt | bilvāraṇyaṃ phalatyadya lokālakṣmīnivṛttaye ‖*)

इति ब्रह्मांडपुराणवचनात् । तत्रत्यबिल्वफलस्नानाशनादिकमलक्ष्मीपरिहारकं भवत्विति भावः ॥ (*iti brahmāṃḍapurāṇavacanāt | tatratyabilvaphalasnānāśanādikamalakṣmīparihārakaṃ bhavatviti bhāvaḥ ‖*)

**Saubhāgya-sañjīvana**

द्वितीयवर्गे प्रथमामृचं जपेत्प्रेणाति बाला तरुणारुणश्रीः । यस्यास्तपोयोगबलेन बिल्वफलान्यलक्ष्मीं शमयंति नृणां ॥ (*dvitīyavarge prathamāmṛcaṃ japetpreṇāti bālā taruṇāruṇaśrīḥ | yasyāstapoyogabalena bilvaphalānyalakṣmīṃ śamayaṃti nṛṇāṃ ‖*)

तनुमध्यां महालक्ष्मीं बालां त्रिपुरसुंदरीं । त्रिबिल्वपत्रैस्संपूज्य सहस्रं प्रजपेद्यचं ॥ तिष्टुप् शिवाक्षरा यस्माच्चिवलक्षजपं चरेत् । बिल्वस्य हविषा होम आज्यहोमश्च शस्यते ॥ ऐश्वर्येणार्चयेद्देवीं बिल्वपत्रैर्हरिद्रया । रुद्रलक्षप्रदीपैश्च नीराजनविधिं चरेत् ॥ पक्वबिल्वफलाहारस्सतं नियतव्रतः । शांतिं कृत्वा विधानेन जगदंबाप्रसादतः ॥ अलक्ष्मीं परिभूयाथ महालक्ष्मीं स्थिरां लभेत् ॥ (*tanumadhyāṃ mahālakṣmīṃ bālāṃ tripurasuṃdarīṃ | tribilvapatraissaṃpūjya sahasraṃ prajapedyacaṃ ‖ tiṣṭup śivākṣarā yasmāccivalakṣajapaṃ caret | bilvasya haviṣā homa ājyahomaśca śasyate ‖ aiśvaryeṇārcayeddevīṃ bilvapatrairharidrayā | rudralakṣapradīpaiśca nīrājanavidhiṃ caret ‖ pakvabilvaphalāhārassataṃ niyatavrataḥ | śāṃtiṃ kṛtvā vidhānena jagadaṃbāprasādataḥ ‖ alakṣmīṃ paribhūyātha mahālakṣmīṃ sthirāṃ labhet ‖*)

ऐं क्लीं सौः ऐं ह्रीं श्रीं। इति बीजषट्केन अनुलोमविलोमपरिवर्तितेन षट्त्रिंशन्ना साः । ध्यानं— (*aiṃ klīṃ sauḥ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ| iti bījaṣaṭkena anulomavilomaparivartitena ṣaṭtriṃśannā sāḥ | dhyānaṃ—*)

उद्यदादित्यसंकाशां बिल्वकाननमध्यगां । शनुमध्यां श्रियं ध्यायेदलक्ष्मीपरिहारिणीं ॥ (*udyadādityasaṃkāśāṃ bilvakānanamadhyagāṃ | śanumadhyāṃ śriyaṃ dhyāyedalakṣmīparihāriṇīṃ ‖*)

अस्य मंत्रस्य विधिवदेकादशलक्षजपात् सारस्वतसिद्धिः । चतुश्चत्वारिंशल्लक्षजपान्महदैश्वर्यभोगभाग्याभिवृद्धिः । तावत्पारायणमात्रेणापि सभक्तिकेन तदुचितफलसिद्धिरिति तत्त्वं । पंचदशाक्षर्यां षष्टो हकारः षष्ठीमिमामृचं लक्षयति । षष्ठ्याः गायत्राः प्रणवार्थाभिव्यंजकत्वेन हार्द्राज्ञाननिवृत्तिद्वारा मोक्षदायकत्वं च सिद्धमिति फलितं ॥ (*asya maṃtrasya vidhivadekādaśalakṣajapāt sārasvatasiddhiḥ | catuścatvāriṃśallakṣajapānmahadaiśvaryabhogabhāgyābhivṛddhiḥ | tāvatpārāyaṇamātreṇāpi sabhaktikena taducitaphalasiddhiriti tattvaṃ | paṃcadaśākṣaryāṃ ṣaṣṭo hakāraḥ ṣaṣṭhīmimāmṛcaṃ lakṣayati | ṣaṣṭhyāḥ gāyatrāḥ praṇavārthābhivyaṃjakatvena hārdrājñānanivṛttidvārā mokṣadāyakatvaṃ ca siddhamiti phalitaṃ ‖*)

**Skānda-purāṇa, Sanatkumāra-saṃhitā, Bilva-māhātmya**

शिवलिंगार्चनोद्योगी जगद्रक्षणदीक्षितः । महाविष्णुस्तपश्चक्रे तस्यैव सहचारिणी ॥ (*śivaliṃgārcanodyogī jagadrakṣaṇadīkṣitaḥ | mahāviṣṇustapaścakre tasyaiva sahacāriṇī ‖*)

### Printed p. 297

महालक्ष्मीस्तपस्तेपे भर्तृसेवापरायणा । तदा बिल्वतरुर्जातो लक्ष्मीदक्षिणहस्ततः ॥ तत्पत्रैरर्चयामास महाविष्णुश्च शंकरं । बिल्वपत्रार्चितस्तुष्टो महादेवो दयानिधिः । सर्वदेवोत्तमत्वं च सर्वस्वातंत्रमेव च । प्रदद् सर्वपूज्यत्वं सर्वसिद्दिं च विष्णवे ॥ (*mahālakṣmīstapastepe bhartṛsevāparāyaṇā | tadā bilvatarurjāto lakṣmīdakṣiṇahastataḥ ‖ tatpatrairarcayāmāsa mahāviṣṇuśca śaṃkaraṃ | bilvapatrārcitastuṣṭo mahādevo dayānidhiḥ | sarvadevottamatvaṃ ca sarvasvātaṃtrameva ca | pradad sarvapūjyatvaṃ sarvasiddiṃ ca viṣṇave ‖*)

श्रीवृक्ष इति विख्यातो बिल्वद्रुर्देवपूजितः । त्रिगुणैस्त्रिदलैः पत्रैस्त्रिमूर्तिप्रीतिदायकः ॥ त्रयिमयोऽयं विख्यातो नीतो देवैश्च नंदनं । कैलासेऽपि च वैकुंठे श्वेतद्वीपे सुरालये ॥ मंदरादिषु पुण्येषु क्षेत्रेषु सकलेष्टपि । पूज्यंते बिल्वतरवः श्रीवृक्षा इति नारद (*śrīvṛkṣa iti vikhyāto bilvadrurdevapūjitaḥ | triguṇaistridalaiḥ patraistrimūrtiprītidāyakaḥ ‖ trayimayo 'yaṃ vikhyāto nīto devaiśca naṃdanaṃ | kailāse 'pi ca vaikuṃṭhe śvetadvīpe surālaye ‖ maṃdarādiṣu puṇyeṣu kṣetreṣu sakaleṣṭapi | pūjyaṃte bilvataravaḥ śrīvṛkṣā iti nārada*)

फलानि श्रीतपोयोगाद्यस्यालक्ष्मिनिरासने । लक्ष्मीप्राप्त पटीयांसि सेव्यंते पुण्यशालिभिः ॥ ज्ञानप्रदमिमं वृक्षं दारिद्रपरिहारकं । श्रीप्रियं योर्चयेद्बिल्वं स नरो भाग्यवान्भवेत् ॥ श्रीयंत्रं बिल्वपत्रैस्तु शिवलिंगमथापि वा । योर्चयेदन्नहं भक्त्या पुरुषार्थान्समश्नुते ॥ (*phalāni śrītapoyogādyasyālakṣminirāsane | lakṣmīprāpta paṭīyāṃsi sevyaṃte puṇyaśālibhiḥ ‖ jñānapradamimaṃ vṛkṣaṃ dāridraparihārakaṃ | śrīpriyaṃ yorcayedbilvaṃ sa naro bhāgyavānbhavet ‖ śrīyaṃtraṃ bilvapatraistu śivaliṃgamathāpi vā | yorcayedannahaṃ bhaktyā puruṣārthānsamaśnute ‖*)

कालिदासकृतदेवीस्तोत्रे च बिल्वार्जनप्राशस्त्रमुक्तं॥ चंडि त्वच्चर णांबुजार्जनविधौ बिल्विदलोल्लुंठनात्तुट्यक्कंटककोटिभिः परिचयं येषां न जग्मुः कराः । ते दंडांकुशचक्रचापकुलिशश्रेणीशतैरंचिता जायंते पृथिवीभुजः कथमिवांभोजप्रभैः पाणिभिः ॥ (*kālidāsakṛtadevīstotre ca bilvārjanaprāśastramuktaṃ‖ caṃḍi tvaccara ṇāṃbujārjanavidhau bilvidalolluṃṭhanāttuṭyakkaṃṭakakoṭibhiḥ paricayaṃ yeṣāṃ na jagmuḥ karāḥ | te daṃḍāṃkuśacakracāpakuliśaśreṇīśatairaṃcitā jāyaṃte pṛthivībhujaḥ kathamivāṃbhojaprabhaiḥ pāṇibhiḥ ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

In the book called Sārasvata-siddhi the meaning is explained thus:

आदित्यवर्णे (*ādityavarṇe*)—she who shines with a bodily lustre of reddish tinge like the young sun that has just risen; as in अरुणां करुणातरंगिताक्षीं (*aruṇāṃ karuṇātaraṃgitākṣīṃ*), the lustre of the young sun is here given as the illustration.

वनस्पतिः बिल्वः (*vanaspatiḥ bilvaḥ*)—अपुष्पाः फलवंतो ये ते वनस्पतयः स्मृताः । (*apuṣpāḥ phalavaṃto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ |*) Ordinarily trees first put forth flowers and afterwards become laden with fruit. But some kinds of tree bear fruit without putting forth flowers. These are called vanaspati. The bilva tree belongs to this

### Printed p. 298

class of vanaspati. In saying that such a bilva tree arose by your grace, the great nature of the goddess is described here. In some places it is said that for this goddess to perform austerity a forest of bilva trees arose on the bank of the river called Nīvā:

तनुमध्या पुरा बाला नीवातटमुपाश्रिता । बिल्वारण्ये तपश्चक्रे लक्ष्मीर्लोकहितार्थिनी ॥ (*tanumadhyā purā bālā nīvātaṭamupāśritā | bilvāraṇye tapaścakre lakṣmīrlokahitārthinī ‖*)

Such is the statement of the Kālikā-purāṇa; that is, in former times Lakṣmī, slender in the waist and a girl, performed austerity for the good of the world in the forest of bilva trees on the bank of the river called Nīvā. As for what the excellence of this bilva tree is, its greatness is set out in the second half of this mantra.

बिल्वनाथप्रियायास्ते नारायण्यास्तपोबलात् । बिल्वारण्यं फलत्यद्य लोकालक्ष्मीनिवृत्तये ॥ (*bilvanāthapriyāyāste nārāyaṇyāstapobalāt | bilvāraṇyaṃ phalatyadya lokālakṣmīnivṛttaye ‖*)

So it is said in the Brahmāṇḍa-purāṇa. That is, by the power of the austerity of the goddess Lakṣmī, the forest of bilva trees called Bilvāraṇya, dear to Īśvara, bears fruit even now for the removal of the poverty and misfortune of the people in the world. The intention is that if one bathes with the fruits of the bilva trees standing in that Bilvāraṇya, eats of them and so on, troubles such as ill fortune and poverty are removed.

In the book called Saubhāgya-sañjīvana:

By reciting the first Ṛk of the second varga of this Śrī-sūkta, that is, the sixth Ṛk of this sūkta, the goddess Śrī, a girl whose body is a mass of splendour like the young sun, is gratified. By the power of that goddess's austerity and yoga, the bilva fruits destroy the sorrow and poverty of men, it is said.

This goddess called Mahālakṣmī shines with a slender body. Famed in the form of a girl and by the name Tripurasundarī, this Mahālakṣmī is to be worshipped with bilva leaves and this Ṛk recited a thousand times. After one lakh of recitations have been made in this way, oblations are to be made in the fire, with the mantra, of bilva leaves, havis (cooked rice) and ghee. The goddess is to be worshipped with many kinds of ornaments and with auspicious materials such as turmeric, and one lakh lamps are to be lit and the camphor-lamp waved. Afterwards the practiser, observing the vow with restraint, eating well-ripened bilva fruits during the recitation, should complete the oblations and the rest with all their parts. If this is done, by the grace of the goddess Śrī, the mother of the world, sorrow, poverty and the like are destroyed and lasting wealth and the like are obtained. During the recitation of this Ṛk

### Printed p. 299

one should make thirty-six nyāsas, reciting in direct and reverse order the six seed-syllables ऐं, क्षीं सौः ऐं ह्रीं श्रीं (*aiṃ, kṣīṃ sauḥ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ*) **NOTE**, and meditate with the verse:

उद्यदादित्यसंकाशां बिल्वकाननमध्यगां । तनुमध्यां श्रियं ध्यायेदलक्ष्मीपरिहारिणीं ॥ (*udyadādityasaṃkāśāṃ bilvakānanamadhyagāṃ | tanumadhyāṃ śriyaṃ dhyāyedalakṣmīparihāriṇīṃ ‖*)

One should meditate with the above verse, whose meaning is: "I meditate on the goddess Śrī, who shines with a bodily lustre like the rising sun, who is in the middle of the forest of bilva trees, who is slender in the waist, and who removes sorrow and poverty." If this mantra is recited according to rule eleven lakhs of times, the grace of the goddess Sarasvatī results. If forty-four lakhs are recited, great wealth, enjoyment, good fortune and the like are obtained. By the mere devout recitation (pārāyaṇa) of the mantra to the number stated above, the same result is attained. The sixth syllable of the Pañcadaśākṣarī great mantra, the syllable ha, points to this sixth Ṛk. Since this sixth Ṛk contains the chief meaning of the Gāyatrī great mantra and of the Praṇava mantra, the fact that the recitation of this Ṛk grants liberation by removing ignorance is established by experience.

In the Sanatkumāra-saṃhitā of the Skānda-purāṇa, in the description of the greatness of the bilva, it is set out thus:

Once upon a time the great Viṣṇu, engaged in the protection of the world, was intent on the worship of the Śiva-liṅga and performed austerity. Mahālakṣmī, the wife of that Viṣṇu, serving her husband, also performed austerity. Then from the right hand of that Lakṣmī the bilva tree arose. Mahāviṣṇu worshipped Śiva with the leaves of that bilva tree. Pleased by the bilva-leaf worship made by Viṣṇu, Mahādeva Śiva, with a gaze moist with compassion, granted Viṣṇu supremacy among all the gods, complete independence, worship by all, and all success. Thus the bilva tree became famed under the name Śrīvṛkṣa, and that bilva tree became worthy of worship even by the gods. Its leaf, made of three leaflets, as a sign of the three guṇas, sattva, rajas and tamas, became pleasing to the three Mūrtis, Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Maheśvara. The three leaflets of its leaf became famed as the form of the three Vedas, Ṛg, Yajus and Sāman. The gods took that tree and planted it in their garden Nandana. Not only that; listen, O Nārada. That bilva tree, famed by the name Śrīvṛkṣa, thriving in Kailāsa, Vaikuṇṭha, Śvetadvīpa and other places, in heaven, in the temples of the gods and in all holy places, is worshipped by all. By the power of the austerity and yoga performed by Lakṣmī, the fruits of this bilva tree attained greatness. Men of merit who desire the grace of the goddess Lakṣmī eat the fruits of this tree.

### Printed p. 300

The greatness of this tree is exceeding. By worshipping it knowledge is attained, and poverty is removed. The man who worships this tree, which is most dear to the goddess Śrī, becomes fortunate; and the man who devoutly worships the Śrī-yantra and the Śiva-liṅga every day with bilva leaves obtains all the aims of man and lives happily.

In the hymn to the Goddess composed by the great poet named Kālidāsa the greatness of this bilva worship is described thus:

चंडि त्वच्चरणांबुजार्चनविधौ बिल्वदल्लोल्लुंठनात् त्र्युट्यत्कंटककोटिभिः परिचयं येषां न जग्मुः कराः । ते दंडांकुशचक्रचापकुलिश श्रेणीशतैरंचिता जायंते पृथिवीभुजः कथमिवांभोजप्रभैः पाणिभिः ॥ (*caṃḍi tvaccaraṇāṃbujārcanavidhau bilvadallolluṃṭhanāt tryuṭyatkaṃṭakakoṭibhiḥ paricayaṃ yeṣāṃ na jagmuḥ karāḥ | te daṃḍāṃkuśacakracāpakuliśa śreṇīśatairaṃcitā jāyaṃte pṛthivībhujaḥ kathamivāṃbhojaprabhaiḥ pāṇibhiḥ ‖*)

O Caṇḍī, when your lotus feet are worshipped, the hands of those who worship devoutly with bilva leaves never come to know the acquaintance of crores of troubles and pains. That is, hands that have worshipped the goddess have no occasion to do hard work. By the greatness of the bilva worship they have performed, those men are born as kings whose hands, soft as lotuses, bear many royal marks such as the staff, the goad, the discus, the bow and the thunderbolt; how this comes about is a wonder.

Here ends the explanation of the meaning of the sixth Ṛk.

#### Ṛk 7

अस्य श्रीश्रीसूक्तसप्तमयङ्महामंत्रस्य । वासुदेव ऋषिः । मणिमालिनी लक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप् छंदः । उं श्रीं बीजं । मं ब्लूं शक्तिः । श्रीं कीलकं । सर्वकाम्यार्थसिद्धये राजराजेश्वरी महालक्ष्मीप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*asya śrīśrīsūktasaptamayaṅmahāmaṃtrasya | vāsudeva ṛṣiḥ | maṇimālinī lakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭup chaṃdaḥ | uṃ śrīṃ bījaṃ | maṃ blūṃ śaktiḥ | śrīṃ kīlakaṃ | sarvakāmyārthasiddhaye rājarājeśvarī mahālakṣmīprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं उपैतु मां-अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं देवसखि-तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं कीर्तिश्च मणिना सह-मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं प्रादुर्भूतोऽस्मि-अनामिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं राष्ट्रेऽस्मिन्-कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं कीर्तिमृद्धिं ददातु मे-करतलकरपृष्टाभ्यां नमः ॥ ओं उपैतु मां-हृदयाय नमः ओं देवसखि-शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं कीर्तिश्च मणिना सह-शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । प्रादुर्भूतोऽस्मि-कवचाय हुं । ओं राष्ट्रेऽस्मिन्-नेत्रत्रयाय नौषट् । ओं कीर्तिमृद्धिं ददातु मे-अस्त्राय फट् । (*oṃ upaitu māṃ-aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ devasakhi-tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ kīrtiśca maṇinā saha-madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ prādurbhūto 'smi-anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ rāṣṭre 'smin-kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ kīrtimṛddhiṃ dadātu me-karatalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ ‖ oṃ upaitu māṃ-hṛdayāya namaḥ oṃ devasakhi-śirase svāhā | oṃ kīrtiśca maṇinā saha-śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | prādurbhūto 'smi-kavacāya huṃ | oṃ rāṣṭre 'smin-netratrayāya nauṣaṭ | oṃ kīrtimṛddhiṃ dadātu me-astrāya phaṭ |*) भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ ध्यानं ॥ राजराजेश्वरीं लक्ष्मीं वरदां (*bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ ‖ rājarājeśvarīṃ lakṣmīṃ varadāṃ*)

### Printed p. 301

मणिमालिनीं । देवीं देवप्रियां कीर्तिं वंदे काम्यार्थसिद्धये ॥ हेमाभां पीतवस्त्रां कुचभरनमितां मध्यदेशे कृशांगीं कांस्यं पुल्लांभोजयुग्मां करकमलयुगे धारयंतीमभीतिं । मुक्ताहारामुदारां मणिमयमकुटां शोभमानां च पद्मां भक्ताभीष्टार्थदानप्रथितगुणगणां नौम्यहं विश्वभूतिं ॥ इति ध्यात्वा ॥ (*maṇimālinīṃ | devīṃ devapriyāṃ kīrtiṃ vaṃde kāmyārthasiddhaye ‖ hemābhāṃ pītavastrāṃ kucabharanamitāṃ madhyadeśe kṛśāṃgīṃ kāṃsyaṃ pullāṃbhojayugmāṃ karakamalayuge dhārayaṃtīmabhītiṃ | muktāhārāmudārāṃ maṇimayamakuṭāṃ śobhamānāṃ ca padmāṃ bhaktābhīṣṭārthadānaprathitaguṇagaṇāṃ naumyahaṃ viśvabhūtiṃ ‖ iti dhyātvā ‖*)

ओं उपैतु मां देवसखः कीर्तिश्च मणिना सह । प्रादुर्भूतोऽस्मि राष्ट्रेऽस्मिन् कीर्तिमृद्धिं ददातु मे ॥ (*oṃ upaitu māṃ devasakhaḥ kīrtiśca maṇinā saha | prādurbhūto 'smi rāṣṭre 'smin kīrtimṛddhiṃ dadātu me ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

हे श्रीः । देवसखः देवो महादेवस्तस्य सखा कुबेरः कीर्तिः कीर्त्यभिमानिनी तदाख्या दक्षकन्या देवता मणिना चिंतामणिना मणिभद्रेण कोशाध्यक्षेण वा सह सहितः अहं अस्मिन् राष्ट्रे जनपदे प्रादुर्भूतः उत्पन्नः अस्मि आसं । स देवसखस्तत्संबंधिसंगत्या कीर्तिं यशः कोशं वा ऋद्धिं सर्वभोग्यसमृद्धिं मे मह्यं ददातु यच्छतु ॥ (*he śrīḥ | devasakhaḥ devo mahādevastasya sakhā kuberaḥ kīrtiḥ kīrtyabhimāninī tadākhyā dakṣakanyā devatā maṇinā ciṃtāmaṇinā maṇibhadreṇa kośādhyakṣeṇa vā saha sahitaḥ ahaṃ asmin rāṣṭre janapade prādurbhūtaḥ utpannaḥ asmi āsaṃ | sa devasakhastatsaṃbaṃdhisaṃgatyā kīrtiṃ yaśaḥ kośaṃ vā ṛddhiṃ sarvabhogyasamṛddhiṃ me mahyaṃ dadātu yacchatu ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Śrī. देवसखः (*devasakhaḥ*) is Kubera, the friend of the god, Mahādeva. कीर्तिः (*kīrtiḥ*) is the goddess of that name, the daughter of Dakṣa, who presides over fame. मणिना (*maṇinā*) means with the wish-fulfilling jewel, or with Maṇibhadra, the treasurer. I, accompanied by these, have arisen in this realm, this country. Through the connection of that friend of the god, may he give me fame, or treasure, and prosperity, the abundance of all that can be enjoyed.

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**

देवसखः कुबेरः कीर्तिः यशश्च मणिना चिंतामणिना सह मामुपैतु मां प्राप्नोतु । अस्मिन् राष्ट्रे मर्त्यलोके प्रादुर्भूतोऽस्मि जातः । त्वयाऽन्वेषणीयस्त्वत्कटाक्षलक्ष्य इति यावत् । स देवो मे मह्यं कीर्तिं दिगंतविश्रांतं यशः युद्धिमष्टैश्वर्यसमृद्धिं च ददातु प्रयच्छतु ॥ (*devasakhaḥ kuberaḥ kīrtiḥ yaśaśca maṇinā ciṃtāmaṇinā saha māmupaitu māṃ prāpnotu | asmin rāṣṭre martyaloke prādurbhūto 'smi jātaḥ | tvayā 'nveṣaṇīyastvatkaṭākṣalakṣya iti yāvat | sa devo me mahyaṃ kīrtiṃ digaṃtaviśrāṃtaṃ yaśaḥ yuddhimaṣṭaiśvaryasamṛddhiṃ ca dadātu prayacchatu ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May Kubera, the friend of the god, and Fame, together with the wish-fulfilling jewel, come to me, reach me. I have arisen in this realm, the world of mortals; that is, I am the target of your sidelong glance, to be sought by you. May that god give me fame, renown reaching to the ends of the quarters, and prosperity of the eight kinds of lordship.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवसखि (*devasakhi*)—Kubera, famed as the friend of Mahādeva, Īśvara (O goddess Śrī)
- कीर्तिश्च (*kīrtiśca*)—and Kīrti, the daughter of Dakṣa, the deity presiding over fame
- मणिना सह (*maṇinā saha*)—together with the wish-fulfilling jewel, or with Maṇibhadra, the treasurer
- मां उपैतु (*māṃ upaitu*)—may he come near me
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- आस्मिन् राष्ट्रे (*āsmin rāṣṭre*)—in this world of mortals or in this country
- प्रादुर्भूतः अस्मि (*prādurbhūtaḥ asmi*)—I have arisen (for the obtaining of your grace), therefore
- स देवसखि (*sa devasakhi*)—Kubera, the friend of Tryambaka
- मे (*me*)—to me
- कीर्ति (*kīrti*)—fame reaching to the ends of the quarters
- ऋद्धिं (*ṛddhiṃ*)—prosperity of all that can be enjoyed
- ददातु (*dadātu*)—may he give

### Printed p. 302

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O goddess Śrī, may Kubera, the friend of Tryambaka, and Kīrti, the daughter of Dakṣa and the deity presiding over fame, together with the wish-fulfilling jewel, or with the god named Maṇibhadra, the treasurer, come near me in order to show me grace. It is for the gaining of your grace that I have arisen in this country. Therefore may Kubera, the friend of the god, bestow on me fame reaching to the ends of the quarters and the prosperity of all that can be enjoyed, and so show me grace.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

आंडवल्ली (*āṃḍavallī*)

हे श्रीः त्वदुपासकोऽहं यस्मादस्मिन् राष्ट्रे मर्त्यलोके प्रादुर्भूतो जातोऽस्मि तस्मादहं मानवजन्मसाधारणानुपपत्तिमसहमानस्तां प्रार्थये द्वितीयत्वात्मनु त्वदाज्ञपो मामुद्धर्तुं देवसखः कुबेरः “ भवाय देवाय नमः” इत्यादौ देवशब्दो महादेवे रूढः तस्य सखा देवसखः राजाहस्सखिभ्यष्टच् । “कुबेरस्त्र्यंबकसख” इत्यभिदानं । (*he śrīḥ tvadupāsako 'haṃ yasmādasmin rāṣṭre martyaloke prādurbhūto jāto 'smi tasmādahaṃ mānavajanmasādhāraṇānupapattimasahamānastāṃ prārthaye dvitīyatvātmanu tvadājñapo māmuddhartuṃ devasakhaḥ kuberaḥ “ bhavāya devāya namaḥ” ityādau devaśabdo mahādeve rūḍhaḥ tasya sakhā devasakhaḥ rājāhassakhibhyaṣṭac | “kuberastryaṃbakasakha” ityabhidānaṃ |*)

कुबेरस्तपसा पूर्वं तोषयामास शंकरं । शिनार्थं तु महालक्ष्मीः कुबेरस्य वशे स्थिता ॥ तस्माल्लक्ष्मीप्रदानाय कुबेरोऽधिकृतोऽभवत् । तस्माद्दि भक्तवात्सल्यं श्रीदेव्या प्रकटीकृतं ॥ अनुगृह्णाति यं यं श्रीस्तं कुबेरोऽनुधावति । (*kuberastapasā pūrvaṃ toṣayāmāsa śaṃkaraṃ | śinārthaṃ tu mahālakṣmīḥ kuberasya vaśe sthitā ‖ tasmāllakṣmīpradānāya kubero 'dhikṛto 'bhavat | tasmāddi bhaktavātsalyaṃ śrīdevyā prakaṭīkṛtaṃ ‖ anugṛhṇāti yaṃ yaṃ śrīstaṃ kubero 'nudhāvati |*)

इति ईशानसंहितावचनात् (*iti īśānasaṃhitāvacanāt*)

कीर्तियोभिमानिनी देवताकीर्तिर्मतिद्युतिः पुष्टिस्समृद्धिस्तुष्टिरेव च । श्रुतिः स्मृतिर्बलं मेधा श्रद्धारोग्यजयादिकाः ॥ देवताशक्तयः सर्वास्तत्तदेवांशगा नृप । महालक्ष्मीमुपासंते तस्याः किंकर्य एव ताः ॥ (*kīrtiyobhimāninī devatākīrtirmatidyutiḥ puṣṭissamṛddhistuṣṭireva ca | śrutiḥ smṛtirbalaṃ medhā śraddhārogyajayādikāḥ ‖ devatāśaktayaḥ sarvāstattadevāṃśagā nṛpa | mahālakṣmīmupāsaṃte tasyāḥ kiṃkarya eva tāḥ ‖*)

इति भार्गवसंहितावचतात् (*iti bhārgavasaṃhitāvacatāt*) कीर्तिर्लक्ष्मीप्रेष्या मणिना चिंतामणिना सह... (*kīrtirlakṣmīpreṣyā maṇinā ciṃtāmaṇinā saha...*)

कामधेनुः कल्पवृक्षस्सुधा चिंतामणिस्तथा । ऐरावतोच्चैश्रवसावप्सरः कौस्तुभेंदवः ॥ महालक्ष्म्यंशगाः सर्वे क्षीरसागरसंभवाः ॥ श्रियमेवोपजीवंति तेषां देवाश्च देवताः ॥ (*kāmadhenuḥ kalpavṛkṣassudhā ciṃtāmaṇistathā | airāvatoccaiśravasāvapsaraḥ kaustubheṃdavaḥ ‖ mahālakṣmyaṃśagāḥ sarve kṣīrasāgarasaṃbhavāḥ ‖ śriyamevopajīvaṃti teṣāṃ devāśca devatāḥ ‖*)

### Printed p. 303

इति सौरसंहितावचनात् (*iti saurasaṃhitāvacanāt*) मामुपैतु कीर्तिमृद्धिं च मे ददातु मत्समीपमागत्य त्वदाज्ञया ते मदिष्टं साधयंत्विति भावः ॥ (*māmupaitu kīrtimṛddhiṃ ca me dadātu matsamīpamāgatya tvadājñayā te madiṣṭaṃ sādhayaṃtviti bhāvaḥ ‖*)

श्रीरत्नकोशे (*śrīratnakośe*)

अनुष्टुभर्चं श्रीसूक्ते सप्तमीं प्रजपेद्बुधः । द्वात्रिंशल्लक्षपर्याप्तो सिद्धिं प्राप्नोति नान्यथा ॥ कुबेराद्यास्तस्य देवाः प्रत्यक्षाः स्युर्न संशयः । चिंतामण्यादिरत्नानि नवापि निधयस्तथा ॥ वशे तस्या भविष्यंति सिद्धमंत्रस्य योगिनः । भूतप्रेतपिशाचादिग्रहपीडानिवारणं ॥ प्रयतः प्रजपेन्मंत्रं रात्रिकाले विशेषतः । दूर्वाभिर्बिल्वपत्रैश्च सकुशैश्च कुशेशय्यैः ॥ सहरिद्राक्षतयवैस्तंडुलैश्च समौक्तिकैः । कुंकुमैरर्चयेद्देवीं प्रसूनैश्च विशेषतः ॥ केतकीकुंदमंदारैस्तुलसीदमनादिभिः । लक्ष्मीनाम्नां सहस्रेण श्रीबीजसहितेन च ॥ श्रीयंत्रं पूजयेन्नित्यं नित्यकर्माविरोधतः । (*anuṣṭubharcaṃ śrīsūkte saptamīṃ prajapedbudhaḥ | dvātriṃśallakṣaparyāpto siddhiṃ prāpnoti nānyathā ‖ kuberādyāstasya devāḥ pratyakṣāḥ syurna saṃśayaḥ | ciṃtāmaṇyādiratnāni navāpi nidhayastathā ‖ vaśe tasyā bhaviṣyaṃti siddhamaṃtrasya yoginaḥ | bhūtapretapiśācādigrahapīḍānivāraṇaṃ ‖ prayataḥ prajapenmaṃtraṃ rātrikāle viśeṣataḥ | dūrvābhirbilvapatraiśca sakuśaiśca kuśeśayyaiḥ ‖ saharidrākṣatayavaistaṃḍulaiśca samauktikaiḥ | kuṃkumairarcayeddevīṃ prasūnaiśca viśeṣataḥ ‖ ketakīkuṃdamaṃdāraistulasīdamanādibhiḥ | lakṣmīnāmnāṃ sahasreṇa śrībījasahitena ca ‖ śrīyaṃtraṃ pūjayennityaṃ nityakarmāvirodhataḥ |*)

प्रजपेत्तुल्यगायत्रीं पुरश्चरणदीक्षितः ॥ सपाशांकुशमायां च सहाकर्षणपल्लवं । स्वाहांतं च नमोंतं च श्रीमंत्रमृचि योजयेत् ॥ सप्रवालाक्षमालाकस्त्रिसंध्यं नियतो जपेत् । धूपयेद्यक्षधूपैश्च घृतदीपैश्च भासयेत् । द्राक्षाफलं च खर्जूरं रंभाक्षीरेकुसंयुतं । मध्वाज्यदाडिमाचूतनारिकेलान्समर्चयेत् ॥ सुवासिनीस्समभ्यर्च्य स्तुत्वाऽन्यैस्तोत्रपाठकैः । हुत्वापामार्गसमिधो धृताहुतिपुरस्सराः ॥ कट्टाम्ललवणादीसि त्यक्त्वा नियतभुग्वती । सार्धद्विवत्सरे सिद्धिं प्राप्पुयान्नात्र संशयः ॥ (*prajapettulyagāyatrīṃ puraścaraṇadīkṣitaḥ ‖ sapāśāṃkuśamāyāṃ ca sahākarṣaṇapallavaṃ | svāhāṃtaṃ ca namoṃtaṃ ca śrīmaṃtramṛci yojayet ‖ sapravālākṣamālākastrisaṃdhyaṃ niyato japet | dhūpayedyakṣadhūpaiśca ghṛtadīpaiśca bhāsayet | drākṣāphalaṃ ca kharjūraṃ raṃbhākṣīrekusaṃyutaṃ | madhvājyadāḍimācūtanārikelānsamarcayet ‖ suvāsinīssamabhyarcya stutvā 'nyaistotrapāṭhakaiḥ | hutvāpāmārgasamidho dhṛtāhutipurassarāḥ ‖ kaṭṭāmlalavaṇādīsi tyaktvā niyatabhugvatī | sārdhadvivatsare siddhiṃ prāppuyānnātra saṃśayaḥ ‖*)

श्रीं क्रों ऐं श्रीं ह्रीं आं ह्रीं क्रों इति बीजनवकं प्रतिना आं ह्रीं क्रों ऐं श्रीं ह्रीं आं ह्रीं क्रों इति बीजनवकं प्रतिन्यासं योजयेत् । अस्य मंत्रस्य कुबेर ऋषिः । मणिमालिनी लक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप् छंदः । श्रीं ब्लूं क्लीं बीजानि । ध्यानं ॥ (*śrīṃ kroṃ aiṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ iti bījanavakaṃ pratinā āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ aiṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ iti bījanavakaṃ pratinyāsaṃ yojayet | asya maṃtrasya kubera ṛṣiḥ | maṇimālinī lakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭup chaṃdaḥ | śrīṃ blūṃ klīṃ bījāni | dhyānaṃ ‖*)

### Printed p. 304

राजराजेश्वरीं लक्ष्मीं वरदां मणिमालिनीं । देवीं देवपि यां कीर्तिं वंदे काम्यार्थसिद्धये ॥ (*rājarājeśvarīṃ lakṣmīṃ varadāṃ maṇimālinīṃ | devīṃ devapi yāṃ kīrtiṃ vaṃde kāmyārthasiddhaye ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

O goddess Śrī, I, your worshipper, desiring your sidelong glance, have been born in this world or in this country. The hardships and poverty that commonly befall men are mine also. Therefore, at your command, may Kubera, the friend of the god, come near me and bestow on me all good fortune, wealth and prosperity, and fame; this is the main purport indicated in this Ṛk.

देवसखि (*devasakhi*)—In the Vedic sentence भवाय देवाय नमः (*bhavāya devāya namaḥ*) the word देवाय (*devāya*) is well known to mean Mahādeva, Īśvara. He who is a friend of this Mahādeva is, according to the lexicon statement कुबेरस्त्र्यंबकसखिः (*kuberastryaṃbakasakhiḥ*), definitely Kubera.

मणिना (*maṇinā*)—The commentators have explained this word as either the jewel called the wish-fulfilling jewel (Cintāmaṇi), which grants all desires, or the god named Maṇibhadra, the lord of the treasury for the wealth that is in Kubera's keeping.

In former times Kubera performed austerity and pleased Śaṅkara. Pleased with his austerity, Mahādeva granted that Lakṣmī, the presiding deity of wealth, should be in Kubera's keeping. From that time it is well known that Lakṣmī, the presiding deity of riches, dwells always in Kubera's house. Therefore in this Ṛk Kubera is praised for the gaining of Lakṣmī's glance. Moreover, there is the statement of the Īśāna-saṃhitā that, out of the affection Lakṣmī has for her devotees, she shows grace to whoever prays to Kubera for welfare.

कीर्तिः (*kīrtiḥ*)—Kīrti means the presiding deity of fame. In the work called the Bhārgava-saṃhitā it is said that the presiding deities of Kīrti, Mati, Dyuti, Puṣṭi, Samṛddhi, Tuṣṭi, Śruti, Smṛti, Bala, Medhā, Śraddhā, Ārogya and Jaya are forms of the power of Mahālakṣmī, each existing as a portion of her, and serve Mahālakṣmī in the spirit of service. Therefore Kīrti is to be understood as the deity presiding over fame, one among the endless powers of Mahālakṣmī.

मणिना (*maṇinā*)—together with the Cintāmaṇi. The priceless jewel called Cintāmaṇi is spoken of in the Saura-saṃhitā:

कामधेनुः कल्पवृक्षः सुधा चिंतामणिथा । ऐरावतोच्चैश्रवसावप्सरः कौस्तुभेंदवः ॥ महालक्ष्म्यंशगाः सर्वे क्षीरसागरसंभवाः । श्रियमेवोपजीवंति तेषां देवाश्च देवताः ॥ (*kāmadhenuḥ kalpavṛkṣaḥ sudhā ciṃtāmaṇithā | airāvatoccaiśravasāvapsaraḥ kaustubheṃdavaḥ ‖ mahālakṣmyaṃśagāḥ sarve kṣīrasāgarasaṃbhavāḥ | śriyamevopajīvaṃti teṣāṃ devāśca devatāḥ ‖*)

### Printed p. 305

The wish-cow, the wish-tree, nectar, the Cintāmaṇi, the elephant named Airāvata, the horse named Uccaiḥśravas, the women called Apsarases, the Kaustubha jewel and the moon: all these arose at the time of the churning of the ocean of milk, are portions of Mahālakṣmī herself, and rest on Mahālakṣmī alone. It is said that the gods of all these are the deities presiding over each of them.

मामुपैतु कीर्तिमृद्धिंच मे ददातु (*māmupaitu kīrtimṛddhiṃca me dadātu*)—The main purport is that Kubera mentioned above, the deity presiding over fame, and the deity presiding over the Cintāmaṇi, all of them, should come near me and bestow on me fame and the prosperity of the fortune of enjoyment.

श्रीतत्त्वकोशे (*śrītattvakośe*)

The seventh Ṛk of the Śrī-sūkta is in the Anuṣṭubh metre. A discerning man should repeat this Ṛk thirty-two lakhs of times. If he does so, the mantra is perfected; if he does not, it is not perfected. And by repeating it in that way, the deities set forth in that Ṛk, Kubera and the others, become visible to the practitioner. The nine treasures, beginning with the Cintāmaṇi, come under his control. The affliction of ghosts, spirits, goblins and seizing planets is removed. This mantra should be repeated especially with discipline, at night. With dūrvā grass, bilva leaves, kuśa, darbha, rice mixed with turmeric as mantra-consecrated grains, and also kuṅkuma, flowers of many kinds, the screw-pine flower, kunda flowers, mandāra flowers, tulasī and davana, after the performance of the daily rites, the Śrī-yantra is to be worshipped with the thousand names of Lakṣmī together with the seed-syllables. The Lakṣmī Gāyatrī mantra also is to be repeated according to the rule of the preliminary service. While repeating the mantra it is to be repeated at the three times of day with a coral rosary, together with the seed-syllables beginning with Pāśa, Aṅkuśa and Māyā, and with the words svāhā and namaḥ added at the end of the mantra. Thereafter, at the time of worship, incense and a lamp lit with cow's ghee are offered, and grapes, dates, bananas, milk, sugar-cane, honey, ghee, pomegranates, mangoes and coconuts are all offered as food. Married women are to be honoured with clothes and ornaments, and the goddess is to be praised with hymns of many kinds. In the fire, with sticks of the apāmārga plant and with ghee, an oblation is to be made with the mantra. The one who repeats the mantra, during the time of the vow, giving up bitter, sour and salty things and so on, and keeping to a measured diet, is to repeat it. If this is done, the mantra is perfected in two and a half years. In this matter there is no doubt.

While repeating this Ṛk, the nyāsa is to be made together with the nine seed-syllables आं ह्रीं क्रों ऐं श्रीं ह्रीं आं ह्रीं क्रों (*āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ aiṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ*). **NOTE** For this mantra Kubera is the seer; Maṇimālinī Lakṣmī is the deity; Anuṣṭubh is the metre; Śrīṃ is the seed, Blūṃ the power, Klīṃ the pin. The meditation verse is:

### Printed p. 306

राजराजेश्वरीं लक्ष्मीं वरदां मणिमालिनीं । देवीं देवप्रियां कीर्तिं वंदे काम्यार्थसिद्धये ॥ (*rājarājeśvarīṃ lakṣmīṃ varadāṃ maṇimālinīṃ | devīṃ devapriyāṃ kīrtiṃ vaṃde kāmyārthasiddhaye ‖*)

The purport of the meditation verse is: I bow, for the accomplishment of my desired aim, to the deity Lakṣmī, who gives to the devotees who worship her a boon according to the wish of each, who is adorned with ornaments made of many jewels, who is dear to Mahādeva, and who is famed by the name आद्यराजराजेश्वरी (*ādyarājarājeśvarī*).

Here ends the explanation of the meaning of the seventh Ṛk.

#### Ṛk 8

अस्य श्रीश्रीसूक्तस्याष्टमयङ्महामंत्रस्य । श्रीमहाविष्णु र्यषिः । सर्वैश्वर्यकारिणी महालक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप् छंदः । क्षुं बीजं । हं शक्तिः । श्रीं कीलकं । श्रीमहालक्ष्मीप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*asya śrīśrīsūktasyāṣṭamayaṅmahāmaṃtrasya | śrīmahāviṣṇu ryaṣiḥ | sarvaiśvaryakāriṇī mahālakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭup chaṃdaḥ | kṣuṃ bījaṃ | haṃ śaktiḥ | śrīṃ kīlakaṃ | śrīmahālakṣmīprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं रं क्षुत्पिपासामलां ज्येष्ठां—अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं ह्रीं अलक्ष्मींशर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं श्रीं नाशयाम्यहं - मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं ह्रीं अभूतिमसमृद्धिं च—अनामिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं रं सर्वान्निर्णुद—कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं रं ह्रीं श्रीं ह्रीं रं मेगृहात्—करतलकरपृष्टाभ्यां नमः । ओं रं क्षुत्तिपासामलां ज्येष्ठां—हृदयाय नमः । ओं ह्रीं अलक्ष्मीं—शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं श्रीं नाशयाम्यहं—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं ह्रीं अभूतिमसमसमृद्धिं च—कवचाय हुं । ओं रं सर्वान्निर्णुद—नेत्रत्रयाय वषट् । ओं रं ह्रीं श्रीं ह्रीं रं मे गृहात्—अस्त्राय फट् । (*oṃ raṃ kṣutpipāsāmalāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ—aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ alakṣmīṃśarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ śrīṃ nāśayāmyahaṃ - madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ abhūtimasamṛddhiṃ ca—anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ raṃ sarvānnirṇuda—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ raṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ raṃ megṛhāt—karatalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ raṃ kṣuttipāsāmalāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ—hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ alakṣmīṃ—śirase svāhā | oṃ śrīṃ nāśayāmyahaṃ—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ hrīṃ abhūtimasamasamṛddhiṃ ca—kavacāya huṃ | oṃ raṃ sarvānnirṇuda—netratrayāya vaṣaṭ | oṃ raṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ raṃ me gṛhāt—astrāya phaṭ |*) भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्धंधः ॥ ध्यानं (*bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digdhaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ*)

ध्यायेद्देवीं महालक्ष्मीं दारिद्रोच्चाटकारिणीं । पंकजासनमध्यॆ तु सुखासीनां शुचिस्मितां ॥ हेमांबरधरां हेमपद्मपीठोपरि स्थितां । मुक्तामाणिक्यवैढूर्योपेतक्षिणितनूपुरां ॥ रत्नबद्धस्रजां चैव रत्नकुंडलमंडितां । विचित्ररत्नवद्भूषां विचित्रावयवैर्युताः ॥ पद्मद्वयधरां चैव वरदाभयधारिणीं । चामराभ्यां वीज्यमानां सितच्छत्रोपशोभितां ॥ शंखपद्मनिधिभ्यां च सेवितां पादमूलतः । (*dhyāyeddevīṃ mahālakṣmīṃ dāridroccāṭakāriṇīṃ | paṃkajāsanamadhyè tu sukhāsīnāṃ śucismitāṃ ‖ hemāṃbaradharāṃ hemapadmapīṭhopari sthitāṃ | muktāmāṇikyavaiḍhūryopetakṣiṇitanūpurāṃ ‖ ratnabaddhasrajāṃ caiva ratnakuṃḍalamaṃḍitāṃ | vicitraratnavadbhūṣāṃ vicitrāvayavairyutāḥ ‖ padmadvayadharāṃ caiva varadābhayadhāriṇīṃ | cāmarābhyāṃ vījyamānāṃ sitacchatropaśobhitāṃ ‖ śaṃkhapadmanidhibhyāṃ ca sevitāṃ pādamūlataḥ |*)

### Printed p. 307

ज्येष्ठा प्रहरणोद्युक्तामसमृद्धिविनाशिनीं ॥ अभूतिहरणां देवीं ध्यायेत्संपत्प्रदायिनीं । अज्ञानपातकतमस्मितिसूर्यरश्मिं दौर्भाग्यभूधरविदारणवज्रमीडे । रोगार्तिघोरफणिमर्दनपक्षिराजं लक्ष्मीपदद्वयमनर्थहरं सुखार्थी ॥ (*jyeṣṭhā praharaṇodyuktāmasamṛddhivināśinīṃ ‖ abhūtiharaṇāṃ devīṃ dhyāyetsaṃpatpradāyinīṃ | ajñānapātakatamasmitisūryaraśmiṃ daurbhāgyabhūdharavidāraṇavajramīḍe | rogārtighoraphaṇimardanapakṣirājaṃ lakṣmīpadadvayamanarthaharaṃ sukhārthī ‖*) **GLOSS**

ओं क्षुत्पिपासामलां ज्येष्ठा मलक्ष्मीं नाशयाम्यहं । अभूतिमसमृद्धिं च सर्वां निर्णुद मे गृहात् ॥ (*oṃ kṣutpipāsāmalāṃ jyeṣṭhā malakṣmīṃ nāśayāmyahaṃ | abhūtimasamṛddhiṃ ca sarvāṃ nirṇuda me gṛhāt ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

क्षुत्पिपासामलामशनायातृष्णामलिनात्मिकां ज्येष्ठां श्रियः प्रागुत्पन्नामित्यर्थः । अलक्ष्मीमश्रियमहं नाशयामि नाशं प्रापयामि । हे श्रीः त्वमभूतिमसंपत्तिं तथा सर्वामसमृद्धिमपि अनभिवृद्धिं मे मम गृहात् निर्णुद निष्कासय ॥ (*kṣutpipāsāmalāmaśanāyātṛṣṇāmalinātmikāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ śriyaḥ prāgutpannāmityarthaḥ | alakṣmīmaśriyamahaṃ nāśayāmi nāśaṃ prāpayāmi | he śrīḥ tvamabhūtimasaṃpattiṃ tathā sarvāmasamṛddhimapi anabhivṛddhiṃ me mama gṛhāt nirṇuda niṣkāsaya ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

क्षुत्पिपासामला (*kṣutpipāsāmalā*) is she whose nature is soiled by hunger and thirst; ज्येष्ठा (*jyeṣṭhā*), the elder, means one who arose before Śrī. अलक्ष्मी (*alakṣmī*) is Aśrī, the absence of Śrī; I destroy her, I bring her to destruction. O Śrī, you drive out from my house misfortune, that is, lack of wealth, and likewise all non-prosperity, that is, lack of growth.

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**

हे अग्ने क्षुत्पिपासामलां क्षुत्तृड्भ्यां मलिनां कृशांगीं ज्येष्ठां श्रियः अग्रजां-क्षीरोदमथनसमये अलक्ष्माः पूर्वमुत्पन्नतया ज्येष्ठतामाहुः । एता दृशीमलक्ष्मीमश्रियमहं नाशयामि । हे महालक्ष्मि त्वमेवं कुरु । अभूतिमनैश्वर्यं सर्वामसमृद्धिं सकलभोग्यवृद्धिराहित्यं च मे गृहात् मम गेहात् निर्णुद उच्चाटय निरासयेत्यर्थः ॥ (*he agne kṣutpipāsāmalāṃ kṣuttṛḍbhyāṃ malināṃ kṛśāṃgīṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ śriyaḥ agrajāṃ-kṣīrodamathanasamaye alakṣmāḥ pūrvamutpannatayā jyeṣṭhatāmāhuḥ | etā dṛśīmalakṣmīmaśriyamahaṃ nāśayāmi | he mahālakṣmi tvamevaṃ kuru | abhūtimanaiśvaryaṃ sarvāmasamṛddhiṃ sakalabhogyavṛddhirāhityaṃ ca me gṛhāt mama gehāt nirṇuda uccāṭaya nirāsayetyarthaḥ ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, क्षुत्पिपासामला (*kṣutpipāsāmalā*) is she who is soiled and emaciated by hunger and thirst; ज्येष्ठा (*jyeṣṭhā*) is the elder sister of Śrī, for at the time of the churning of the ocean of milk Alakṣmī arose first, and so they call her the eldest. Such an Alakṣmī, an Aśrī, I destroy. O Mahālakṣmī, you act accordingly: drive out from my house, that is, banish and cast away, misfortune, which is lack of lordship, and all non-prosperity, which is the lack of increase of everything enjoyable.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हे अग्ने (*he agne*)—O Agni
- क्षुत्पिपासामलां (*kṣutpipāsāmalāṃ*)—she who is emaciated and soiled by hunger and thirst
- ज्येष्ठां (*jyeṣṭhāṃ*)—she who (at the time of the churning of the ocean) arose before Lakṣmī
- अलक्ष्मीं (*alakṣmīṃ*)—the goddess of poverty
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- नाशयामि (*nāśayāmi*)—I destroy (therefore, O Agni)
- अभूतिं (*abhūtiṃ*)—lack of lordship
- असमृद्धिं (*asamṛddhiṃ*)—the absence of prosperity, that is, poverty
- सर्वां (*sarvāṃ*)—all this
- मे (*me*)—my
- गृहात् (*gṛhāt*)—from the house
- निर्णुद (*nirṇuda*)—strike and drive away

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni (or O goddess Śrī), she who is worn out and emaciated by hunger and thirst, who has a filthy body, and who, since she arose before Lakṣmī at the time of the churning of the ocean of milk, to Lakṣmī

### Printed p. 308

the elder sister, the deity presiding over poverty, I destroy. O Agni, thereafter drive out of my house the low state of being without wealth, and poverty.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

लक्ष्मीयामळे (*lakṣmīyāmaḷe*)

प्रजपेद्विधिना नित्यं श्री सूक्ते चर्चमष्टमीं । प्रत्यक्षरसहस्रं च गायत्रीमपि तावतीं ॥ श्रीगायत्रीदशांशं तु लक्ष्मीमंत्रं शतांशकं । अनिष्टपरिहारार्थो मंत्रोऽयं परिकीर्तितः ॥ दौर्भाग्यं शमयेदेव दुर्लिपिं परिमार्जयेत् । वीरासनसमासीनः अग्निहोत्रपुरस्थितः । फलाशी प्रजपेन्मंत्रं भक्त्या रुद्राक्षमालया । तिलहोमं प्रकुर्वीत विरजामपि हावयेत् ॥ धृतेन जुहुयालक्ष्मीं दौर्भाग्यं तस्य नश्यति । सौभाग्यं जायते तस्य नात्र कार्या विचारणा ॥ (*prajapedvidhinā nityaṃ śrī sūkte carcamaṣṭamīṃ | pratyakṣarasahasraṃ ca gāyatrīmapi tāvatīṃ ‖ śrīgāyatrīdaśāṃśaṃ tu lakṣmīmaṃtraṃ śatāṃśakaṃ | aniṣṭaparihārārtho maṃtro 'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ ‖ daurbhāgyaṃ śamayedeva durlipiṃ parimārjayet | vīrāsanasamāsīnaḥ agnihotrapurasthitaḥ | phalāśī prajapenmaṃtraṃ bhaktyā rudrākṣamālayā | tilahomaṃ prakurvīta virajāmapi hāvayet ‖ dhṛtena juhuyālakṣmīṃ daurbhāgyaṃ tasya naśyati | saubhāgyaṃ jāyate tasya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ‖*)

रं ह्रीं श्रीं ह्रीं रं इति बीजपंचकेन समंत्रांगन्यासः ॥ (*raṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ raṃ iti bījapaṃcakena samaṃtrāṃganyāsaḥ ‖*)

अज्ञानपातकतमस्ततितीव्ररश्मिं दौर्भाग्यभूधरविदारणवज्रमीडे । रोगार्तिघोरफणिमर्दनपक्षिराजं लक्ष्मीपदद्वयमनर्थहरं सुखार्थी ॥ खड्गं सवातचक्रं च कमलं वरमेव च । करैश्चतुर्भिर्बिभ्राणां ध्यायेच्चंद्राननां श्रियं ॥ (*ajñānapātakatamastatitīvraraśmiṃ daurbhāgyabhūdharavidāraṇavajramīḍe | rogārtighoraphaṇimardanapakṣirājaṃ lakṣmīpadadvayamanarthaharaṃ sukhārthī ‖ khaḍgaṃ savātacakraṃ ca kamalaṃ varameva ca | karaiścaturbhirbibhrāṇāṃ dhyāyeccaṃdrānanāṃ śriyaṃ ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

In the work called Lakṣmī-yāmaḷa it is said thus. The eighth Ṛk in the Śrī-sūkta is to be repeated every day according to the rule. It is to be repeated as many thousand times as there are syllables in the mantra. After that the Lakṣmī Gāyatrī mantra is to be repeated to the extent of one hundredth part of the number stated above, and the Śrī Gāyatrī mantra to the extent of one tenth part. It is well known that by repeating this mantra misfortune, poverty and other evils are removed. The man who repeats it, after performing the agnihotra and other rites, is to sit in the hero posture (vīrāsana) facing the agnihotra fire, hold in his hand a rosary of rudrākṣa beads, and repeat the mantra with devotion. On the day of the repetition he is to keep to a measured diet, eating only fruits and the like. After the repetition he is to make a sesame oblation and a virajā oblation in the fire. If an oblation of ghee is made with the mantra, addressed to the goddess Lakṣmī, that man's sorrow and poverty are destroyed, and

### Printed p. 309

all wealth and good fortune come to him. There is no need to doubt this. While repeating this mantra, the nyāsa is to be made together with the five seed-syllables रं ह्रीं श्रीं ह्रीं रं (*raṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ raṃ*), and the meditation verses given below are to be recited and meditated on.

अज्ञानपातकतमस्ततीतीव्ररश्मिं दौर्भाग्यभूधरविदारणवज्रमीडे । रोगार्तिघोरफणिमर्दनपक्षिराजं लक्ष्मीपदद्वयमनर्थहरं सुखार्थी ॥ खड्गं सवातचक्रं च कमलं वरमेव च । करैश्चतुर्भिर्बिभ्राणां ध्यायेच्चंद्राननां श्रियं ॥ (*ajñānapātakatamastatītīvraraśmiṃ daurbhāgyabhūdharavidāraṇavajramīḍe | rogārtighoraphaṇimardanapakṣirājaṃ lakṣmīpadadvayamanarthaharaṃ sukhārthī ‖ khaḍgaṃ savātacakraṃ ca kamalaṃ varameva ca | karaiścaturbhirbibhrāṇāṃ dhyāyeccaṃdrānanāṃ śriyaṃ ‖*)

The pair of feet of the goddess Śrī Lakṣmī is like the sharp rays of the sun in driving away the darkness called ignorance and sin; it is like the thunderbolt that splits the mountain called misfortune; it is like Garuḍa, the king of birds, who destroys the terrible serpents called disease and affliction. I praise the pair of feet of Śrī Lakṣmī, who has the power and capacity to remove such evils. This goddess Śrī, holding in her four hands a sword, a discus, a lotus and the hand granting a boon, shines with a radiance pleasing to the eye like the moon. Such a goddess Śrī is to be meditated on. This is the main purport of the meditation verses.

Here ends the explanation of the meaning of the eighth Ṛk.

#### Ṛk 9 (continued)

[अस्य श्रीश्रीसूक्तनवमऋचो गंधद्वारेति महामंत्रस्य । श्रीमहाविष्णुर्यषिः । श्रीमहालक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप्छंदः ॥ गं बीजं । ह्रीं शक्तिः । श्रीं कीलकं । श्रीमहालक्ष्मीप्रसाद सिद्ध्यर्थे गंधद्वारेति मंत्रजपे विनियोगः ॥ (*[asya śrīśrīsūktanavamaṛco gaṃdhadvāreti mahāmaṃtrasya | śrīmahāviṣṇuryaṣiḥ | śrīmahālakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭupchaṃdaḥ ‖ gaṃ bījaṃ | hrīṃ śaktiḥ | śrīṃ kīlakaṃ | śrīmahālakṣmīprasāda siddhyarthe gaṃdhadvāreti maṃtrajape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं गंधद्वारां—अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं दुराधर्षां—तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं नित्यपुष्टां—मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं करीषिणीं—अनामिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं ईश्वरीं सर्वभूतानां—कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं तामिहोपह्वये श्रियं—करतलकरपृष्ठाभ्यां नमः । (*oṃ gaṃdhadvārāṃ—aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ durādharṣāṃ—tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ—madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ karīṣiṇīṃ—anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ īśvarīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyaṃ—karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ |*) ओं गंधद्वारां—हृदयाय नमः । ओं दुराधर्षां—शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं नित्यपुष्टां—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं करीषिणीं—कवचाय हुं ॥ ओं ईश्वरीं सर्वभूतानां—नेत्रत्रयाय वौषट् । ओं तामिहोपह्वये श्रियं— अस्त्राय फट् ॥ (*oṃ gaṃdhadvārāṃ—hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ durādharṣāṃ—śirase svāhā | oṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ karīṣiṇīṃ—kavacāya huṃ ‖ oṃ īśvarīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ—netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | oṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyaṃ— astrāya phaṭ ‖*) भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ अथ ध्यानं ॥ (*bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖ atha dhyānaṃ ‖*)

### Printed p. 310

कल्पद्रोः पादमूले द्रुतकनकनिभाचारुसिंहासनस्थां हस्तैः पद्मौ वरदमभयं धारयंतीं च मालां । नानाभूषां सुवेषां मणिमकुटधरां दिव्यगंधानुलेपां ध्यायेत्संपूर्णकामप्रदकरकमलां कंबुकंठीं हृदब्बे ॥ एवं ध्यात्वा जपेन्मंत्रं लक्षं सर्वार्थसिद्धये] ॥ (*kalpadroḥ pādamūle drutakanakanibhācārusiṃhāsanasthāṃ hastaiḥ padmau varadamabhayaṃ dhārayaṃtīṃ ca mālāṃ | nānābhūṣāṃ suveṣāṃ maṇimakuṭadharāṃ divyagaṃdhānulepāṃ dhyāyetsaṃpūrṇakāmapradakarakamalāṃ kaṃbukaṃṭhīṃ hṛdabbe ‖ evaṃ dhyātvā japenmaṃtraṃ lakṣaṃ sarvārthasiddhaye] ‖*) **GLOSS**

गंधद्वारां दुराधर्षां नित्यपुष्टां करीषिणीं । ईश्वरीं सर्वभूतानां तामिहोपह्वये श्रियं ॥ (*gaṃdhadvārāṃ durādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīṃ | īśvarīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyaṃ ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

गंधः घ्राणग्राह्यो गुणः द्वारं लक्षणं यस्यास्तां दुराधर्षां केनापि धर्षितुमशक्यां नित्यपुष्टां नित्यं सदा सस्यादिभिः पुष्टां समृद्धां करीषिणीं करीषः शुष्कगोमयादिस्तद्वतीं गवाश्वादिबहुपशुसमृद्धामिति यावत् । सर्वभूतानां सकलप्राणिनामीश्वरीमधिष्ठात्रीमाधारभूतां ना तां भूरूपां श्रियमिह उपह्वये उपाह्वये । (*gaṃdhaḥ ghrāṇagrāhyo guṇaḥ dvāraṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yasyāstāṃ durādharṣāṃ kenāpi dharṣitumaśakyāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ nityaṃ sadā sasyādibhiḥ puṣṭāṃ samṛddhāṃ karīṣiṇīṃ karīṣaḥ śuṣkagomayādistadvatīṃ gavāśvādibahupaśusamṛddhāmiti yāvat | sarvabhūtānāṃ sakalaprāṇināmīśvarīmadhiṣṭhātrīmādhārabhūtāṃ nā tāṃ bhūrūpāṃ śriyamiha upahvaye upāhvaye |*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

गंधद्वारा (*gaṃdhadvārā*) is she whose mark (dvāra) is smell, the quality grasped by the nose. दुराधर्षा (*durādharṣā*) is she who cannot be overcome by anyone. नित्यपुष्टा (*nityapuṣṭā*) is she who is ever nourished by crops and the like, that is, prosperous. करीषिणी (*karīṣiṇī*) is she who has karīṣa, dried cow-dung and the like; that is, she is rich in many animals such as cows and horses. She is the ruler, the presiding deity and the support of all beings, all living creatures. Her, Śrī in the form of earth, I here invite. **NOTE**

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**

हे अग्ने गंधद्वारां गंधवतीं पृथ्वीरूपां दुराधर्षां दुस्सहां केनापि धर्षितुमशक्यामित्यर्थः । नित्यपुष्टां नित्यं सस्यादिभिः पोषणशीलां करीषिणीं शुष्कगोमयवतीं अनेन पृथिव्या असंख्यातपशुमत्तं व्यज्यते । सर्वभूतानां समस्त प्राणिनामीश्वरीं नियामिकां प्रभुरूपां तां श्रियं लक्ष्मीमिह आस्मिन् देशे अहमुपह्वये मत्समीपं प्रत्यागच्छेत्याह्वानं करोमीत्यर्थः ॥ विष्णुपति ० महा देवीमिति भूसूक्ते । श्रीर्भूर्निलीला रमा दुर्गा सीता सत्या च भामिनी । रुक्ष्मिणीतुलसीराधास्सर्वा लक्ष्म्यंशसंभवाः ॥ इति श्रीरहस्ये ॥ (*he agne gaṃdhadvārāṃ gaṃdhavatīṃ pṛthvīrūpāṃ durādharṣāṃ dussahāṃ kenāpi dharṣitumaśakyāmityarthaḥ | nityapuṣṭāṃ nityaṃ sasyādibhiḥ poṣaṇaśīlāṃ karīṣiṇīṃ śuṣkagomayavatīṃ anena pṛthivyā asaṃkhyātapaśumattaṃ vyajyate | sarvabhūtānāṃ samasta prāṇināmīśvarīṃ niyāmikāṃ prabhurūpāṃ tāṃ śriyaṃ lakṣmīmiha āsmin deśe ahamupahvaye matsamīpaṃ pratyāgacchetyāhvānaṃ karomītyarthaḥ ‖ viṣṇupati 0 mahā devīmiti bhūsūkte | śrīrbhūrnilīlā ramā durgā sītā satyā ca bhāminī | rukṣmiṇītulasīrādhāssarvā lakṣmyaṃśasaṃbhavāḥ ‖ iti śrīrahasye ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, गंधद्वारा (*gaṃdhadvārā*) is she who has smell, who is in the form of earth. दुराधर्षा (*durādharṣā*) is she who is hard to bear, that is, whom no one can overcome. नित्यपुष्टा (*nityapuṣṭā*) is she who is ever nourishing with crops and the like. करीषिणी (*karīṣiṇī*) is she who has dried cow-dung; by this the possession of countless cattle by the earth is shown. She is the ruler of all beings, their controller, of the form of the lord. That Śrī, Lakṣmī, I here, in this country, invite: I make the invitation, "Come back to my presence." He cites, with the Bhū-sūkta, the words विष्णुपति ० महा देवीमिति भूसूक्ते (*viṣṇupati 0 mahā devīmiti bhūsūkte*) **NOTE**, and a verse from the Śrī-rahasya naming Śrī, Bhū, Nīlā, Ramā, Durgā, Sītā, Satyā, Bhāminī, Rukmiṇī, Tulasī and Rādhā as all born of portions of Lakṣmī.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हे अग्ने (*he agne*)—O Agni
- गंधद्वारां (*gaṃdhadvārāṃ*)—she whose mark is the quality of smell, that is, who has the form of earth
- दुराधर्षां (*durādharṣāṃ*)—who cannot be borne by anyone

### Printed p. 311

- नित्यपुष्टां (*nityapuṣṭāṃ*)—who is at all times nourished together with crops and the like, that is, endowed with abundance of crops
- करीषिणीं (*karīṣiṇīṃ*)—endowed with dried cow-dung, that is, endowed with the wealth of countless cattle
- सर्वभूतानां (*sarvabhūtānāṃ*)—of all living beings
- ईश्वरीं (*īśvarīṃ*)—who is the mistress
- तां (*tāṃ*)—her who is in the form of earth
- श्रियं (*śriyaṃ*)—the goddess Śrī
- इह (*iha*)—to the place where I am
- उप ह्वये (*upa hvaye*)—I call, praying

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, I pray and call to my presence the goddess Śrī, who has the form of earth, whose mark is smell; whose shape or weight cannot be borne by anyone; who at all times nourishes all with crops and the like and with countless wealth of cattle; and who, in the form of earth, is the lord and controller of all living beings.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

वैखानसविद्यायां (*vaikhānasavidyāyāṃ*)

ऋचमानुष्टुभीं गंधद्वारामिति जपेच्छ्रियः । षष्टुत्तरशतानां तु सहस्राणां च संख्यया ॥ पद्माक्षमालया पद्मासनस्थसह पार्थिवं । बीजं विन्यस्य मंत्रेण श्रीबीजेन च तज्ञपेत् ॥ केसरीचंद्रकस्तूरीश्रीखंडाद्यैर्मनोहरैः । गंधद्रव्यैस्समभ्यर्च्य स्थंडिले श्रियमर्चयेत् ॥ (*ṛcamānuṣṭubhīṃ gaṃdhadvārāmiti japecchriyaḥ | ṣaṣṭuttaraśatānāṃ tu sahasrāṇāṃ ca saṃkhyayā ‖ padmākṣamālayā padmāsanasthasaha pārthivaṃ | bījaṃ vinyasya maṃtreṇa śrībījena ca tajñapet ‖ kesarīcaṃdrakastūrīśrīkhaṃḍādyairmanoharaiḥ | gaṃdhadravyaissamabhyarcya sthaṃḍile śriyamarcayet ‖*)

गोवृंदानुगतां ध्यात्वा सुरभिं सस्यमालिनीं । पृथुलां प्राणिनामीशां दुर्धर्षां श्रियमर्चयेत् ॥ (*govṛṃdānugatāṃ dhyātvā surabhiṃ sasyamālinīṃ | pṛthulāṃ prāṇināmīśāṃ durdharṣāṃ śriyamarcayet ‖*)

कृषिफलप्रदः धनधान्यपशुसमृद्धिकरः पुण्यगंधप्रदः महत्वापादकः प्रभुत्वसाधकश्च मंत्रोऽयं ॥ (*kṛṣiphalapradaḥ dhanadhānyapaśusamṛddhikaraḥ puṇyagaṃdhapradaḥ mahatvāpādakaḥ prabhutvasādhakaśca maṃtro 'yaṃ ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

In this Ṛk the goddess Śrī is praised, conceived as being in the form of earth. In this matter it is said in the work called Śrī-rahasya:

श्रीर्भूर्निलीला रमा दुर्गा सीता सत्या च भामिनी । (*śrīrbhūrnilīlā ramā durgā sītā satyā ca bhāminī |*)

रुक्ष्मिणीतुलिसीराधाः सर्वा लक्ष्म्यंशसंभवाः ॥ (*rukṣmiṇītulisīrādhāḥ sarvā lakṣmyaṃśasaṃbhavāḥ ‖*)

That is, since it is said that all the goddesses, Śrīdevī, Bhūdevī (Pṛthivī), Nīlā, Ramā, Durgā, Sītā, Satyabhāmā, Rukmiṇī, Tulasī and Rādhā, are born of portions of the goddess Lakṣmī, in this Ṛk the goddess Śrī is described as bearing the form of earth.

### Printed p. 312

गंधद्वारां (*gaṃdhadvārāṃ*)—गंधः घ्राणग्राह्यो गुणः द्वारं लक्षणं यस्यास्तां (*gaṃdhaḥ ghrāṇagrāhyo guṇaḥ dvāraṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yasyāstāṃ*). Earth and the other five elements have the chief qualities of sound, touch and so on. Thus, it is said in the Vedic sentences that sound is the chief quality of space, touch of air, form of fire, taste of water, and smell of earth. Therefore here, when earth is designated, its quality, smell, is stated as its mark. For this reason it is customary to say गंधवती पृथिवी (*gaṃdhavatī pṛthivī*).

दुराधर्षां (*durādharṣāṃ*)—Since earth, unlike other ordinary things, is very large in size and extremely heavy, the idea that no one is able to bear (carry) it is indicated by the epithet दुराधर्षां (*durādharṣāṃ*).

नित्यपुष्टां (*nityapuṣṭāṃ*)—Since on the earth crops, trees and plants always grow abundantly, and nourishing food for all living beings is obtained from them, the earth is described as nourishing everyone.

करीषिणीं (*karīṣiṇīṃ*)—शुष्कगोमयस्तद्वतीं (*śuṣkagomayastadvatīṃ*). The commentator has explained करीष (*karīṣa*) as dried cow-dung. Where there is cow-dung there must be cattle. Therefore "one who has dried cow-dung" means one who has the wealth of cattle, the cows and so on, that are the cause of that cow-dung. The idea is that for living beings or for people to get nourishing food, abundance of crops and abundance of cattle must exist. The earth is praised as one that possesses these two kinds of nourishment.

In the work called Vaikhānasa-vidyā the manner in which this Ṛk is to be repeated is described thus. The Ṛk of the Śrī-sūkta in the Anuṣṭubh metre beginning गंधद्वारां (*gaṃdhadvārāṃ*) is to be repeated one lakh sixty thousand times. The practitioner, seated in the lotus posture, holding in his hand the rosary called padmākṣa, is to make the nyāsa together with the earth seed-syllable and the Śrī seed-syllable, and repeat this Ṛk. Having properly made a sthaṇḍila (ritual platform), decorated it with rangoli and the like, and installed the goddess Śrī upon it, he is to worship her with saffron, camphor, musk, sandal, and charming, fragrant auspicious substances, meditating on the goddess Śrī as being in the form of earth, in which there is a multitude of cows and fragrant abundance of crops, who is very extensive, the mistress of all living beings, and possessed of incomparable power and capacity that no one can endure. By worshipping the goddess in this manner and repeating this mantra, the fruit of cultivation, that is, grain and other produce of agriculture, wealth and abundance of cattle, all this will be obtained. Welfare, regard, lordship and similar distinctions will also be obtained.

Here ends the explanation of the ninth Ṛk.

### Printed p. 313

#### Ṛk 10

[अस्य श्री श्रीसूक्तमनसःकाममिति दशमयङ्महामंत्रस्य । काम ऋषिः । श्रीमहालक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप्छंदः । मं बीजं । शं शक्तिः । श्रीः कीलकं । महालक्ष्मीप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*[asya śrī śrīsūktamanasaḥkāmamiti daśamayaṅmahāmaṃtrasya | kāma ṛṣiḥ | śrīmahālakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭupchaṃdaḥ | maṃ bījaṃ | śaṃ śaktiḥ | śrīḥ kīlakaṃ | mahālakṣmīprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं ओं मनसः काममाकूतिं—अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं ऐं वाचस्सत्यं— तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं लं अशीमहि—मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं वं पशूनां रूपमन्नस्य—अनामिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं श्रीं मयिश्रीः—कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं ओं ऐं लं वं श्रीं श्रयतां यशः—करतलकरपृष्ठाभ्यां नमः । (*oṃ oṃ manasaḥ kāmamākūtiṃ—aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ aiṃ vācassatyaṃ— tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ laṃ aśīmahi—madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ vaṃ paśūnāṃ rūpamannasya—anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ śrīṃ mayiśrīḥ—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ oṃ aiṃ laṃ vaṃ śrīṃ śrayatāṃ yaśaḥ—karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ |*) ओं ओं मनसः काममाकूतिं—हृदयाय नमः । ओं ऐं वाचस्सत्यं—शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं लं अशीमहि— शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं वं पशूनां रूपमन्नस्य—कवचाय हुं । ओं श्रीं मयि श्रीः— नेत्रत्रयाय नौषट् । ओं ओं ऐं लं वं श्रीं श्रयतां यशः—अस्त्राय फट् ॥ (*oṃ oṃ manasaḥ kāmamākūtiṃ—hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ aiṃ vācassatyaṃ—śirase svāhā | oṃ laṃ aśīmahi— śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ vaṃ paśūnāṃ rūpamannasya—kavacāya huṃ | oṃ śrīṃ mayi śrīḥ— netratrayāya nauṣaṭ | oṃ oṃ aiṃ laṃ vaṃ śrīṃ śrayatāṃ yaśaḥ—astrāya phaṭ ‖*) भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्धंधः ॥ ध्यानं ॥ (*bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digdhaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ ‖*) कांत्या कांचनसन्निभां द्विनयनां कल्हारमालावतीं कस्तूरीतिलकां करैश्च कमले कंठेन मुक्तावळीं । शेषाशेषविशेषभूषणरतां संबिभ्रतीं कुंडलां पद्माक्षीं भज सर्वदाभिलषितप्राप्त्यै समृद्धश्रियै ॥ एवं ध्यात्वा जपेन्मंत्रलक्षमात्रमतंद्रितः ॥ (*kāṃtyā kāṃcanasannibhāṃ dvinayanāṃ kalhāramālāvatīṃ kastūrītilakāṃ karaiśca kamale kaṃṭhena muktāvaḷīṃ | śeṣāśeṣaviśeṣabhūṣaṇaratāṃ saṃbibhratīṃ kuṃḍalāṃ padmākṣīṃ bhaja sarvadābhilaṣitaprāptyai samṛddhaśriyai ‖ evaṃ dhyātvā japenmaṃtralakṣamātramataṃdritaḥ ‖*) **GLOSS**

मनसः काममाकूतिं वाचः सत्यमशीमहि । पशूनां रूपमन्नस्य मयि श्रीः श्रयतां यशः ॥ (*manasaḥ kāmamākūtiṃ vācaḥ satyamaśīmahi | paśūnāṃ rūpamannasya mayi śrīḥ śrayatāṃ yaśaḥ ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

हे श्रीः मनसः कामं मनोरथमाकूतिं संकल्पं वाचः वागिंद्रियस्य सत्यं याथार्थ्यं पशूनां गोमहिष्यादीनां रूपं क्षीरादि अन्नस्य अदनीयस्य यवप्रीह्यादेः रूपं !भक्ष्यादिचतुर्विधमशीमहि लभेमहि । श्रीः संपत् यशः कीर्तिश्च मयि त्वदुश्वासके श्रयतां आश्रयतां ॥ (*he śrīḥ manasaḥ kāmaṃ manorathamākūtiṃ saṃkalpaṃ vācaḥ vāgiṃdriyasya satyaṃ yāthārthyaṃ paśūnāṃ gomahiṣyādīnāṃ rūpaṃ kṣīrādi annasya adanīyasya yavaprīhyādeḥ rūpaṃ !bhakṣyādicaturvidhamaśīmahi labhemahi | śrīḥ saṃpat yaśaḥ kīrtiśca mayi tvaduśvāsake śrayatāṃ āśrayatāṃ ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Śrī, may we obtain: the काम (*kāma*) of the mind, that is, the wish; the आकूति (*ākūti*), that is, the resolve; the सत्य (*satya*) of speech, that is, the truthfulness of the organ of speech; the form of cattle, that is, of cows, buffaloes and the like, namely milk and so on; the form of food, that is, of what is to be eaten, barley, rice and the like, the fourfold food beginning with what is chewed. May Śrī, wealth, and यशः (*yaśaḥ*), fame, abide in me, your worshipper.

### Printed p. 314

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**

मम मनसः कामं भूरिमनोरथमाकूतिं संतोषं वाचस्सत्यं वचसां यथार्थतां पशूनां रूपं गजाश्वगोमहिष्यादिप्राचुर्यमन्नस्य रूपं भक्ष्यभोज्यादिकं यशः कीर्तिं च यस्यास्सकाशात् वयमशीमहि लभेमहि । तादृशी श्रीलक्ष्मीः मयि श्रयतां सन्निधत्तां ॥ (*mama manasaḥ kāmaṃ bhūrimanorathamākūtiṃ saṃtoṣaṃ vācassatyaṃ vacasāṃ yathārthatāṃ paśūnāṃ rūpaṃ gajāśvagomahiṣyādiprācuryamannasya rūpaṃ bhakṣyabhojyādikaṃ yaśaḥ kīrtiṃ ca yasyāssakāśāt vayamaśīmahi labhemahi | tādṛśī śrīlakṣmīḥ mayi śrayatāṃ sannidhattāṃ ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Of my mind, काम (*kāma*) is abundance of wishes; आकूति (*ākūti*) is contentment; the सत्य (*satya*) of speech is the correctness of words; the form of cattle is abundance of elephants, horses, cows, buffaloes and the like; the form of food is what is to be chewed and eaten and the like; यशः (*yaśaḥ*) is fame. From whose presence we may obtain these, such a Śrī, Lakṣmī, may she abide in me, may she be present.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मनसः (*manasaḥ*)—of the mind
- कामं (*kāmaṃ*)—the desired object
- आकूतिं (*ākūtiṃ*)—resolve, or joy
- वाचः (*vācaḥ*)—of words
- सत्यं (*satyaṃ*)—truth
- पशूनां रूपं (*paśūnāṃ rūpaṃ*)—wealth of cattle in the form of cows, buffaloes and the like
- अन्नस्य (रूपं) (*annasya (rūpaṃ)*)—the heaps of grains such as barley and rice, needed for food and other nourishment
- आशीमहि (*āśīmahi*)—may we obtain
- श्रीः (*śrīḥ*)—wealth and other prosperity
- यशः (*yaśaḥ*)—fame
- मयि (*mayi*)—in me
- श्रयतां (*śrayatāṃ*)—may it abide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May we obtain, by the grace of the goddess Śrī, all the desires of our minds, the fulfilment of our resolves, satisfaction, truthfulness in our speech, wealth of cattle such as cows and buffaloes, and abundance of grains such as rice and barley; and may abundance of wealth and fame abide in me.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

मंत्रकल्पार्णवे (*maṃtrakalpārṇave*)

मनसः काममित्याद्यां श्रीसूक्ते दशमीमृचं । मनोवाङ्मयतो नित्यमष्टलक्षजपं चरेत् ॥ वाक्सिद्धिर्भोगसिद्धिश्च भोग्यलाभो भवेद्भवं ॥ (*manasaḥ kāmamityādyāṃ śrīsūkte daśamīmṛcaṃ | manovāṅmayato nityamaṣṭalakṣajapaṃ caret ‖ vāksiddhirbhogasiddhiśca bhogyalābho bhavedbhavaṃ ‖*)

ओं ऐंलं वं श्रीमिति सपल्लवं न्यासः (*oṃ aiṃlaṃ vaṃ śrīmiti sapallavaṃ nyāsaḥ*)

तां ध्यायेत्सत्यसंकल्पां लक्ष्मीं क्षीरोदनप्रियां । ख्यातां सर्वॆषु भूतेषु तत्त्वज्ञा नबलक्रियां ॥ (*tāṃ dhyāyetsatyasaṃkalpāṃ lakṣmīṃ kṣīrodanapriyāṃ | khyātāṃ sarvèṣu bhūteṣu tattvajñā nabalakriyāṃ ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

The mantra मनसःकाममाकूतिं (*manasaḥkāmamākūtiṃ*), the tenth Ṛk of the Śrī-sūkta, is to be repeated every day with purity of mind until the number reaches eight lakhs. If this is done, success in speech, success in enjoyment, and the wealth that is the means of enjoyment will all certainly be obtained. While repeating this mantra, the nyāsa is to be made with the seed-syllables ओं ऐं लं वं श्रीं (*oṃ aiṃ laṃ vaṃ śrīṃ*), and the repetition done. The meditation is to be done in this way.

### Printed p. 315

तां ध्यायेत्सत्यसंकल्पां लक्ष्मीं क्षीरोदनप्रियां । ख्यातां सर्वेषु भूतेषु तत्त्वज्ञानबलक्रियां ॥ (*tāṃ dhyāyetsatyasaṃkalpāṃ lakṣmīṃ kṣīrodanapriyāṃ | khyātāṃ sarveṣu bhūteṣu tattvajñānabalakriyāṃ ‖*)

Meditating according to this verse, the repetition is to be made. The main purport of the meditation verse is: I meditate on the goddess Śrī, who holds firm to her resolve of truth, who is fond of rice boiled in milk, and who, dwelling in all living beings as their inner self, stirs up in them knowledge, strength and action.

#### Ṛk 11

अस्य श्री श्रीसूक्तस्थ कर्दमेनेत्येकादशऋग्महामंत्रस्य । कर्दम ऋषिः । महालक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप्छंदः । कं बीजं । वं शक्तिः । श्रीं कीलकं । महालक्ष्मीप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*asya śrī śrīsūktastha kardamenetyekādaśaṛgmahāmaṃtrasya | kardama ṛṣiḥ | mahālakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭupchaṃdaḥ | kaṃ bījaṃ | vaṃ śaktiḥ | śrīṃ kīlakaṃ | mahālakṣmīprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं कर्दमेन प्रजाभूता-अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं मयि संभव-तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं कर्दम-मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं श्रियं वासय मे कुले-अनामिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं मातरं-कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं पद्ममालिनीं-करतलकरपृष्ठाभ्यां नमः । (*oṃ kardamena prajābhūtā-aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ mayi saṃbhava-tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ kardama-madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule-anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ mātaraṃ-kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ padmamālinīṃ-karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ |*) ओं कर्दमेन प्रजाभूता-हृदयाय नमः । ओं मयि संभव-शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं कर्दम-शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं श्रियं वासय मे कुले-कवचाय हुं । ओं मातरंनेत्रत्रयाय वषट् । ओं पद्ममालिनीं-अस्त्राय फट् । भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ ध्यानं ॥ (*oṃ kardamena prajābhūtā-hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ mayi saṃbhava-śirase svāhā | oṃ kardama-śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule-kavacāya huṃ | oṃ mātaraṃnetratrayāya vaṣaṭ | oṃ padmamālinīṃ-astrāya phaṭ | bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ ‖*) ध्यायेद्धाटकसंनिभां द्विनयनां दिव्यांबरालंकृतां । सत्फुल्लोदरपद्मकोमलयुग श्रीमत्करांभोरुहां ॥ नानारत्नकिरीटकुंडललसद्वक्रांबुजां पद्मिनीं । सर्वाभीष्टफलप्रदाननिरतां संपत्समृद्धै सदा ॥ ध्यात्वा जपेल्लक्षं महाकाव्येयुतं जपेत् ॥ (*dhyāyeddhāṭakasaṃnibhāṃ dvinayanāṃ divyāṃbarālaṃkṛtāṃ | satphullodarapadmakomalayuga śrīmatkarāṃbhoruhāṃ ‖ nānāratnakirīṭakuṃḍalalasadvakrāṃbujāṃ padminīṃ | sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradānaniratāṃ saṃpatsamṛddhai sadā ‖ dhyātvā japellakṣaṃ mahākāvyeyutaṃ japet ‖*) **GLOSS**

कर्दमेन प्रजाभूता मयि संभव कर्दम । श्रियं वासय मे कुले मातरं पद्ममालिनीं ॥ (*kardamena prajābhūtā mayi saṃbhava kardama | śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule mātaraṃ padmamālinīṃ ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

कर्दमेन कर्दमाख्यपुत्रेण प्रजाभूता प्रकृष्टं प्रजा अपत्यं यस्याः सा सपुत्रेत्यर्थः ॥ “ प्राजापत्यमिति ” यास्कवचनात् । भूता अभवत् । आतः हे कर्दम (*kardamena kardamākhyaputreṇa prajābhūtā prakṛṣṭaṃ prajā apatyaṃ yasyāḥ sā saputretyarthaḥ ‖ “ prājāpatyamiti ” yāskavacanāt | bhūtā abhavat | ātaḥ he kardama*)

### Printed p. 316

श्रीपुत्र त्वं मयि मदीयगृहे संभव संवस । पद्ममालिनीं कमलमालाधारिणीं मातरं तव जननीं श्रियं लक्ष्मीं मे मम कुले वंशे वासय निवासय ॥ (*śrīputra tvaṃ mayi madīyagṛhe saṃbhava saṃvasa | padmamālinīṃ kamalamālādhāriṇīṃ mātaraṃ tava jananīṃ śriyaṃ lakṣmīṃ me mama kule vaṃśe vāsaya nivāsaya ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

कर्दमेन (*kardamena*): by the son named Kardama, प्रजाभूता (*prajābhūtā*) is she who has excellent offspring (प्रजा (*prajā*)), that is, she has a son; this is according to the statement of Yāska, प्राजापत्यमिति (*prājāpatyamiti*). भूता (*bhūtā*) means she became. Therefore, O Kardama, son of Śrī, dwell in me, in my house. पद्ममालिनीं (*padmamālinīṃ*) is she who wears a garland of lotuses; your mother Śrī, Lakṣmī, make dwell, cause to abide, in my family, my lineage.

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**
