हे कर्दम श्रीपुत्र मयि संभव मयि प्रसादं कुरु । यतः श्रीः त्वया कर्दमेन प्रजाभूता पुत्रवती जाता । तस्मान्मातरं तव जननीं पद्मिनीं पद्मरचितमालिकां श्रियं लक्ष्मीं मम कुले वंशे वासय प्रतिष्ठापय । त्वत्परा सती वासं करोश्वित्यर्थः ॥ (*he kardama śrīputra mayi saṃbhava mayi prasādaṃ kuru | yataḥ śrīḥ tvayā kardamena prajābhūtā putravatī jātā | tasmānmātaraṃ tava jananīṃ padminīṃ padmaracitamālikāṃ śriyaṃ lakṣmīṃ mama kule vaṃśe vāsaya pratiṣṭhāpaya | tvatparā satī vāsaṃ karośvityarthaḥ ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Kardama, son of Śrī, मयि संभव (*mayi saṃbhava*): show favour to me. Since Śrī has become प्रजाभूता (*prajābhūtā*), a mother of a son, through you, Kardama, therefore make your mother, पद्मिनीं (*padminīṃ*), she who has a garland made of lotuses, Śrī, Lakṣmī, dwell in my family, establish her there. The meaning is: being devoted to you, may she make her dwelling.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- कर्दमेन (*kardamena*)—by the son named Kardama (the goddess Śrī)
- प्रजाभूता (*prajābhūtā*)—has become one who has a son (therefore)
- कर्दम (*kardama*)—O Kardama, son of Śrī (you)
- मयि (*mayi*)—in me or in my house
- संभव (*saṃbhava*)—dwell, or show grace to me
- पद्ममालिनीं (*padmamālinīṃ*)—who wears a garland strung of lotus flowers
- मातरं श्रियं (*mātaraṃ śriyaṃ*)—the goddess Śrī, your mother
- मे (*me*)—my
- कुले (*kule*)—among those born in my family, or in my family
- वासय (*vāsaya*)—cause to dwell, impel to dwell

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This goddess Śrī, having obtained a son named Kardama, is famed on that account as one who has a son. O Kardama, son of Śrī, dwell in my house and show grace to me; and the goddess Śrī, adorned with a garland of lotus flowers and your mother, impel or favour to dwell in my family.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

मंत्रकल्पार्णवे (*maṃtrakalpārṇave*)

कर्दमेनेत्यादिकां तु श्रीसूक्तैकादशीमृचं । गावं सवत्सां संपूज्य जपेदक्षरलक्षकं ॥ (*kardamenetyādikāṃ tu śrīsūktaikādaśīmṛcaṃ | gāvaṃ savatsāṃ saṃpūjya japedakṣaralakṣakaṃ ‖*)

वं श्रीं वमिति न्यासः (*vaṃ śrīṃ vamiti nyāsaḥ*)

सवत्सा गौरिव प्रीता कर्दमेन तथेंदिरा । कल्याणी मद्गृहे नित्यं निवसेत्पद्ममालिनी ॥ (*savatsā gauriva prītā kardamena tatheṃdirā | kalyāṇī madgṛhe nityaṃ nivasetpadmamālinī ‖*)

मंत्रसिद्धौ तु चिराविच्छिन्नवंशपरंपराभिवृद्धिरखंडितैश्वर्यलाभश्च भवति । संतानसौभाग्यप्रदोऽयं मंत्रः । पद्माक्षमालया गोष्ठे प्रजपेत् । बालेभ्यः पयो दद्याद्वालिकाभ्यश्च प्रत्यहं सुवासिनीमर्चयेत्सपुत्रामिति ॥ (*maṃtrasiddhau tu cirāvicchinnavaṃśaparaṃparābhivṛddhirakhaṃḍitaiśvaryalābhaśca bhavati | saṃtānasaubhāgyaprado 'yaṃ maṃtraḥ | padmākṣamālayā goṣṭhe prajapet | bālebhyaḥ payo dadyādvālikābhyaśca pratyahaṃ suvāsinīmarcayetsaputrāmiti ‖*)

### Printed p. 317

##### Anuvāda

This mantra beginning कर्दमेन (*kardamena*) is the eleventh Ṛk in the Śrī-sūkta. The Ṛk is to be repeated after worshipping a cow with her calf. It is to be repeated as many lakhs of times as there are syllables in the mantra. Before the repetition the nyāsa is to be made with the seed-syllables वं श्रीं वं (*vaṃ śrīṃ vaṃ*), and then the repetition is to be done. As a cow with her calf looks lovingly at her calf and rejoices, so the goddess Śrī also is pleased when she is in company with her son Kardama. The main purport is: may the goddess Śrī, wearing a garland of lotus flowers and shining with divine ornaments, dwell always in my house, bringing about auspiciousness.

If this mantra is repeated and the mantra is perfected, a lineage that is never broken and the attainment of unbroken lordship will be obtained. The repetition of this mantra gives the good fortune of offspring. Seated in the cow-pen where cows are tied, with a rosary of padmākṣa beads as the rosary for repetition, the mantra is to be repeated. Thereafter milk is to be given to boys and girls. And every day married women who have small children, that is, women with sons, are to be honoured with clothes, ornaments and the like.

Here ends the explanation of the eleventh Ṛk.

#### Ṛk 12

अस्य श्रीश्रीसूक्तस्य आपःसृजंत्विति द्वादशऋङ्महामंत्रस्य । चिक्लीतो भगवानृषिः । अमृतेश्वरी देवता । अनुष्टुप् छंदः । आं बीजं । लं शक्तिः । श्रीं कीलकं । चिक्लीतमातृश्री लक्ष्मीदेवताप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*asya śrīśrīsūktasya āpaḥsṛjaṃtviti dvādaśaṛṅmahāmaṃtrasya | ciklīto bhagavānṛṣiḥ | amṛteśvarī devatā | anuṣṭup chaṃdaḥ | āṃ bījaṃ | laṃ śaktiḥ | śrīṃ kīlakaṃ | ciklītamātṛśrī lakṣmīdevatāprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं आपःसृजंतु स्निग्धानि—अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं चिक्लीत वस—तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं मे गृहे—मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं नि च देवीं मातरं—अनामिकाभ्या नमः । ओं श्रियं वासय—कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं मे कुले—करतलकरपृष्टाभ्यां नमः । (*oṃ āpaḥsṛjaṃtu snigdhāni—aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ ciklīta vasa—tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ me gṛhe—madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ ni ca devīṃ mātaraṃ—anāmikābhyā namaḥ | oṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ me kule—karatalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |*) ओं आपःस्रजंतु स्निग्धानि—हृदयाय नमः । ओं चिक्लीत वस—शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं मे गृहे—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं नि च देवीं मातरं—कवचाय हुं । ओं श्रियं वासय—नेत्रत्रयाय वौषट् । ओं मे कुले—अस्त्राय फट् ॥ भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः । ध्यानं ॥ (*oṃ āpaḥsrajaṃtu snigdhāni—hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ ciklīta vasa—śirase svāhā | oṃ me gṛhe—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ ni ca devīṃ mātaraṃ—kavacāya huṃ | oṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya—netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | oṃ me kule—astrāya phaṭ ‖ bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ | dhyānaṃ ‖*) ध्यायेत्कल्पतरोर्मूले रत्नसिंहासने स्थितां । पद्मद्वयधरां पद्मां वरदाभयधारिणीं ॥ सर्वरत्नविचित्रांगीं रक्तश्रीपादपीठिकां । हेमदंडसितच्छत्रचामरद्वयवीजितां ॥ (*dhyāyetkalpatarormūle ratnasiṃhāsane sthitāṃ | padmadvayadharāṃ padmāṃ varadābhayadhāriṇīṃ ‖ sarvaratnavicitrāṃgīṃ raktaśrīpādapīṭhikāṃ | hemadaṃḍasitacchatracāmaradvayavījitāṃ ‖*)

### Printed p. 318

रत्नाघ्नरभिषिंचंतीं भक्तंगजकरोद्भृतैः । एवं ध्यात्वा जपेल्लक्षं भवेद्धनपतिःस्वयं ॥ (*ratnāghnarabhiṣiṃcaṃtīṃ bhaktaṃgajakarodbhṛtaiḥ | evaṃ dhyātvā japellakṣaṃ bhaveddhanapatiḥsvayaṃ ‖*) **GLOSS**

आपः सृजंतु स्निग्धानि चिक्लीत वस मे गृहे । नि च देवीं मातरं श्रियं वासय मे कुले ॥ (*āpaḥ sṛjaṃtu snigdhāni ciklīta vasa me gṛhe | ni ca devīṃ mātaraṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

आपः अबभिमानिन्नो देवताः स्निग्धानि स्नेहयुक्तानि कार्याणि सृजंतु उद्वादयंतु । अपां स्नेहगुणवत्त्वादिति भावः । हे चिक्लीत चिक्लीताख्य श्रिपुत्र मे मम गृहे गेहे निवस च वासं कुरु । अपि च तां देवीं मातरं श्रियं मे मम कुले निवासय संवासय ॥ (*āpaḥ ababhimāninno devatāḥ snigdhāni snehayuktāni kāryāṇi sṛjaṃtu udvādayaṃtu | apāṃ snehaguṇavattvāditi bhāvaḥ | he ciklīta ciklītākhya śriputra me mama gṛhe gehe nivasa ca vāsaṃ kuru | api ca tāṃ devīṃ mātaraṃ śriyaṃ me mama kule nivāsaya saṃvāsaya ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

आपः (*āpaḥ*) are the presiding deities of the waters; may they create, bring forth, स्निग्धानि (*snigdhāni*), things endowed with affection (sneha); the idea is that the waters possess the quality of affection. O Ciklīta, son of Śrī, dwell in my house. And further, make that goddess, your mother Śrī, dwell, abide, in my family.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- चिक्लीत (*ciklīta*)—O sage Ciklīta, son of the goddess Śrī
- मे (*me*)—my
- गृहे (*gṛhe*)—in the house
- वस (*vasa*)—dwell
- च (*ca*)—and
- देवीं (*devīṃ*)—who is radiant, or who is endowed with qualities such as play
- मातरं (*mātaraṃ*)—your mother
- श्रियं (*śriyaṃ*)—the goddess Śrī
- मे (*me*)—my
- कुले (*kule*)—in the family
- निवासय (*nivāsaya*)—impel to dwell

**NOTE**

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

सूक्तार्थसंग्रहे (*sūktārthasaṃgrahe*)

यदग्रे आप एवासन्नद्यस्सर्वमभूज्जगत् । नारास्ता अयनं यस्य स हि नारायणः परः ॥ सूते नरांश्च या आपस्ता यदस्यायनं हरेः । तस्य स्वाभाविकी शक्तिर्देवी नारायणी स्मृता ॥ अद्भो हि पृथिवी जाता अग्निरप्यभवत्तथा । वायुरर्कॊ ह्यपां वत्सश्चंद्रमास्सर्वमप्यथ ॥ जीवं जीवरसं दिव्यममृतं जलमुच्यते । जायते लीयते यत्र जगत्तज्वलमारितं ॥ तज्जलासिच शांतात्मा चिरं जलमुपासते । तस्मात्सर्वनिदानानामपां सृष्टत्वमुच्यते ॥ (*yadagre āpa evāsannadyassarvamabhūjjagat | nārāstā ayanaṃ yasya sa hi nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ ‖ sūte narāṃśca yā āpastā yadasyāyanaṃ hareḥ | tasya svābhāvikī śaktirdevī nārāyaṇī smṛtā ‖ adbho hi pṛthivī jātā agnirapyabhavattathā | vāyurarkò hyapāṃ vatsaścaṃdramāssarvamapyatha ‖ jīvaṃ jīvarasaṃ divyamamṛtaṃ jalamucyate | jāyate līyate yatra jagattajvalamāritaṃ ‖ tajjalāsica śāṃtātmā ciraṃ jalamupāsate | tasmātsarvanidānānāmapāṃ sṛṣṭatvamucyate ‖*)

### Printed p. 319

अप्पु प्रतिष्ठितः स्नेहस्तेनैव परमाणवः । जगद्भावाय संबद्धास्तद्दानौ स्याज्जगल्लयः ॥ तस्मादुपासको देवीं प्रार्थयेदस्वरूपिणीं । भोग्यद्रव्याणि सर्वाणि मम स्निग्धानि मातृका ॥ (*appu pratiṣṭhitaḥ snehastenaiva paramāṇavaḥ | jagadbhāvāya saṃbaddhāstaddānau syājjagallayaḥ ‖ tasmādupāsako devīṃ prārthayedasvarūpiṇīṃ | bhogyadravyāṇi sarvāṇi mama snigdhāni mātṛkā ‖*)

सृजतु श्रीरपां देवी भोगभावाय भामिनी । श्रीदेव्यास्तनयो जज्ञॆ चिक्लीतो नाम मन्मथः ॥ अबन्नविकृतौ चित्ते जातः कामो हि चित्रजः । आयमेव हि संसारहेतुः सर्वार्थसाधकः ॥ (*sṛjatu śrīrapāṃ devī bhogabhāvāya bhāminī | śrīdevyāstanayo jajñè ciklīto nāma manmathaḥ ‖ abannavikṛtau citte jātaḥ kāmo hi citrajaḥ | āyameva hi saṃsārahetuḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ ‖*)

जगत्कामहितं सर्वं येन दैतफलोदयः । यश्च कामहतो देही श्रोत्रियो ब्रह्मणि स्थितः ॥ ब्रह्म संपत्सते तस्य कर्तव्यं न हि विद्यते । लक्ष्म्यास्त्रिवर्गरूपायाः प्राप्तिः कामनिबंधना ॥ (*jagatkāmahitaṃ sarvaṃ yena daitaphalodayaḥ | yaśca kāmahato dehī śrotriyo brahmaṇi sthitaḥ ‖ brahma saṃpatsate tasya kartavyaṃ na hi vidyate | lakṣmyāstrivargarūpāyāḥ prāptiḥ kāmanibaṃdhanā ‖*)

तस्माच्चिक्षीतनामानं लक्ष्मीपुत्रं हि दुर्जयं । कामदेवं समाराध्य यत्नतः प्रापुयाच्छियं ॥ लक्ष्मीः प्रयत्नसाध्या हि यत्नः कामसमुद्भवः । तस्य धर्माविरुद्धस्य विभूतित्वं हरेद्यतः ॥ (*tasmāccikṣītanāmānaṃ lakṣmīputraṃ hi durjayaṃ | kāmadevaṃ samārādhya yatnataḥ prāpuyācchiyaṃ ‖ lakṣmīḥ prayatnasādhyā hi yatnaḥ kāmasamudbhavaḥ | tasya dharmāviruddhasya vibhūtitvaṃ haredyataḥ ‖*)

चिक्लीत श्रीसुत स्वामिंश्चिरं निवस मद्गृहे । मत्कुले सुचिरं तिष्ठ मच्चित्ते संनिधिं कुरु ॥ त्वदागमनमात्रेण त्वन्माता त्वामनुव्रजेत् । त्वयि प्रीतिपरा सा हि तवच्छंदोनुवर्तिनी ॥ श्रीदेवीं तां मम गृहे चिरं वासय मातरं । नमोऽस्तु तुभ्यं चिक्लीत श्रीदेव्यै च नमो नमः ॥ (*ciklīta śrīsuta svāmiṃściraṃ nivasa madgṛhe | matkule suciraṃ tiṣṭha maccitte saṃnidhiṃ kuru ‖ tvadāgamanamātreṇa tvanmātā tvāmanuvrajet | tvayi prītiparā sā hi tavacchaṃdonuvartinī ‖ śrīdevīṃ tāṃ mama gṛhe ciraṃ vāsaya mātaraṃ | namo 'stu tubhyaṃ ciklīta śrīdevyai ca namo namaḥ ‖*)

एतत्तात्पर्यकां सूक्ते प्रजपेद्दादशीमृचं । चंद्रमा ऋषिरेतस्य देवता त्वम्यतेश्वरी ॥ चिक्लीतमाता श्रीश्चंदोनुष्टुब्बं बीजमुच्यते । चतुःषष्ठि सहस्राणि प्रजपेन्मंत्रसिद्धये ॥ (*etattātparyakāṃ sūkte prajapeddādaśīmṛcaṃ | caṃdramā ṛṣiretasya devatā tvamyateśvarī ‖ ciklītamātā śrīścaṃdonuṣṭubbaṃ bījamucyate | catuḥṣaṣṭhi sahasrāṇi prajapenmaṃtrasiddhaye ‖*)

नद्यासमुद्रगामिन्नास्तिरे बिल्वतरोस्टटे । जलकुंभं समभ्यर्च्य चेष्टिं चाभीष्टकां चरेत् ॥ यच्च याजुषशाखायामुक्तमारुणकेतुके । तेन मंत्रेण सहितं स्वशाखोक्तविधिं चरेत् ॥ प्रपादानं प्रकुर्वित दद्यात्पानीयमेव च । (*nadyāsamudragāminnāstire bilvatarosṭaṭe | jalakuṃbhaṃ samabhyarcya ceṣṭiṃ cābhīṣṭakāṃ caret ‖ yacca yājuṣaśākhāyāmuktamāruṇaketuke | tena maṃtreṇa sahitaṃ svaśākhoktavidhiṃ caret ‖ prapādānaṃ prakurvita dadyātpānīyameva ca |*)

### Printed p. 320

सुवासिनीभ्यो विप्रेभ्यस्तदपत्येभ्य एव च ॥ भूताप्स्यायनतो देवी तुष्यति श्रीर्हरिप्रिया । तस्यान्नं भोग्यजातं च तदिच्छासमकालिकं ॥ हितं मृष्टं समृद्धं च शुद्धं यावदपेक्षितं । तत्कुटुंबस्य पर्याप्तं तदाश्रितजनस्य च ॥ तदावासे तत्कुले च महालक्ष्मीः स्थिरा भवेत् ॥ (*suvāsinībhyo viprebhyastadapatyebhya eva ca ‖ bhūtāpsyāyanato devī tuṣyati śrīrharipriyā | tasyānnaṃ bhogyajātaṃ ca tadicchāsamakālikaṃ ‖ hitaṃ mṛṣṭaṃ samṛddhaṃ ca śuddhaṃ yāvadapekṣitaṃ | tatkuṭuṃbasya paryāptaṃ tadāśritajanasya ca ‖ tadāvāse tatkule ca mahālakṣmīḥ sthirā bhavet ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

According to the Vedic statement आपो ना इदमग्रे सलिलमासीत् (*āpo nā idamagre salilamāsīt*), in the beginning water covered the whole universe; there was no other thing. From that water this whole visible world arose. Water is named नाराः (*nārāḥ*), as in आपो नारा इति प्रोक्ताः (*āpo nārā iti proktāḥ*). The god for whom this water was the dwelling-place came to be named Nārāyaṇa. And those waters, the dwelling-place of that Nārāyaṇa, produced narān, that is, human beings; that is, from water alone the whole world, beginning with human beings, came into being. Therefore those waters called नाराः (*nārāḥ*), the cause of the origin of the world, became, under the name Nārāyaṇī, the natural power of Nārāyaṇa, the goddess. Then from the water principle, from water, as in the Vedic statement अद्भ्यः पृथिवी (*adbhyaḥ pṛthivī*), the earth arose. Then likewise the origin of fire, air, sun, moon and the others took place. Since from this water, the nectar that is the sap sustaining life arose, water came to be named jala. Or, since this whole world of moving and unmoving things is born from this water and dissolves in water itself, water came to be named jala. A man purified by such water, tranquil in spirit, worships the water principle. Therefore, since water is the cause of everything, it is said that water has the power of creation. In water there is the quality called sneha (the property of sticking together). Therefore the atoms, the basic supports of the world, united with one another by the power of sneha of that water, became the cause of the origin of the world. In the end, by the absence of sneha, it is likewise the cause of the dissolution of the world; therefore the goddess is to be worshipped. The goddess is to be meditated on and worshipped as being in the form of the power of water, that is, in the form of water. One should pray that the goddess, who has the form of water and is the mother of the world, may create for my enjoyment all the objects of enjoyment that are most dear to me. To this goddess Śrī a son named चिक्षीत (*cikṣīta*) was born. They call him Manmatha. It has been said before that the chief quality of water is sneha. Since this son of the goddess Śrī, who has the form of water, named Cikṣīta, arose from the mind of the goddess, which is a modification of water, he came to have the names Cittaja and Kāma. **NOTE** The desire that appears in the mind is itself

### Printed p. 321

the cause of worldly existence. It is also the accomplisher of every purpose, and it gives room in people's minds to the sense of duality and so is the cause of the dealings of the world. That is to say, a man joined with desire (kāma; the word also means wishing) is engaged in the dealings of the world, and this worldly activity is itself the sense of duality. But the man free of desire, devoid of the sense of duality, attains the non-dual understanding that everything is Brahman alone and that there is no thing apart from Brahman; he is called a Śrotriya and remains absorbed in meditation on Brahman. In this world he has no duty or rite that he must perform. The attainment of the goddess Śrī Lakṣmī, who has the form of the three aims of life, has to come through her son Kāma, or Cikīta. Therefore, if one worships with effort the son of Lakṣmī, named Cikīta, who is famed as the god of desire (Kāmadeva) hard to conquer, and wins his favour, the favour of the goddess Śrī also follows and wealth is gained. To win Lakṣmī over is possible by effort, and that effort must itself come from Kāmadeva, the son of Lakṣmī. Therefore one should gratify the son of Lakṣmī and the goddess Lakṣmī by effort and practice not contrary to dharma, and obtain the fullness of wealth and prosperity.

O Cikkīta, son of Śrī, O lord, dwell in my house for a long time. Abide in my family at all times. Abide in my mind with a gracious disposition. If you come to my house, your mother, the goddess Śrī, out of love for you, will follow you and come along with you. Impel your mother, that goddess Śrī, to dwell in my house for a long time. O Cikkīta, here is a salutation to you, and many salutations to your mother, the goddess Śrī.

The twelfth Ṛk of the Śrīsūkta, which contains the meaning and purport stated above, is to be repeated with a disciplined mind. The seer of this Ṛk is Candra; the deity is the goddess Śrī, the mother of Cikṣīta; the metre is Anuṣṭubh; the seed (bīja) is नं (*naṃ*). For the mantra to be accomplished it must be repeated sixty-four thousand times. On the bank of a river that flows to the sea, or under a bilva tree, in a clean place, one should set up a pot full of water, invoke the deity in it, and worship according to rule. One should follow the rules and injunctions stated in the mantras called Āruṇaketaka in the Yajus branch, and perform the worship in the manner stated by one's own branch, according to the procedure (tantra) stated there. After the worship one should invite Brāhmaṇas, married women (suvāsinī) and their children, and satisfy them by giving drinks such as sweet syrup (pānaka). By this satisfaction of beings the goddess Śrī, dear to Hari, becomes pleased. For the man who performs the worship and the rest in this way, and for the members of his family and his dependants, wholesome, abundant and pure delicious food and many kinds of enjoyable things are obtained in as much plenty as he desires. Mahālakṣmī dwells firmly in his house and in his family.

Here ends the explanation of the twelfth Ṛk.

### Printed p. 322

#### Ṛk 13

अस्य श्रीश्रीसूक्तस्य आद्र्रां पुष्करिणीमिति त्रयोदशऋग्महामंत्रस्य । जातवेदा ऋषिः । श्री महालक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप् छंदः । आं बीजं । स्वाहा शक्तिः । श्रीं कीलकं । श्रीमहालक्ष्मीप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*asya śrīśrīsūktasya ādrrāṃ puṣkariṇīmiti trayodaśaṛgmahāmaṃtrasya | jātavedā ṛṣiḥ | śrī mahālakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭup chaṃdaḥ | āṃ bījaṃ | svāhā śaktiḥ | śrīṃ kīlakaṃ | śrīmahālakṣmīprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं आद्र्रा० पुष्करिणीं पुष्टिं-अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं पिंगळां-तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं पद्ममालिनीं-मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं चंद्रां हिरण्मयीं लक्ष्मींअनामिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं जातवेदः-कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं म आवह-करतलकरपृष्ठाभ्यां नमः । (*oṃ ādrrā0 puṣkariṇīṃ puṣṭiṃ-aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ piṃgaḷāṃ-tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ padmamālinīṃ-madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ caṃdrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃanāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ jātavedaḥ-kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ ma āvaha-karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ |*) ओं आद्र्रा० पुष्करिणीं पुष्टिं-हृदयाय नमः । ओं पिंगळां-शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं पद्ममालिनीं-शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं चंद्रां हिरण्मयीं लक्ष्मीं-कवचाय हुं । ओं जातवेदः-नेत्रत्रयाय वषट् । ओं म आ वह-अस्त्राय फट् ॥ भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ ध्यानं ॥ (*oṃ ādrrā0 puṣkariṇīṃ puṣṭiṃ-hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ piṃgaḷāṃ-śirase svāhā | oṃ padmamālinīṃ-śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ caṃdrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ-kavacāya huṃ | oṃ jātavedaḥ-netratrayāya vaṣaṭ | oṃ ma ā vaha-astrāya phaṭ ‖ bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ ‖*) ध्यायेत्कल्पतरोर्मूले रत्नसिंहासने स्थितां । पद्मद्वयधरां पद्मां वरदाभयधारिणीं ॥ सर्वरत्नविचित्रांगीं रक्तश्रीपादपीठिकां । नियतः प्रजपेन्मंत्री लक्षसंख्या जपेन्मनुं । पिपासानाशनं बुद्धिदायकं प्रथमं स्मृतं । अभूतिमसमृद्धिं च द्वितीयेन विनाशयेत् ॥ (*dhyāyetkalpatarormūle ratnasiṃhāsane sthitāṃ | padmadvayadharāṃ padmāṃ varadābhayadhāriṇīṃ ‖ sarvaratnavicitrāṃgīṃ raktaśrīpādapīṭhikāṃ | niyataḥ prajapenmaṃtrī lakṣasaṃkhyā japenmanuṃ | pipāsānāśanaṃ buddhidāyakaṃ prathamaṃ smṛtaṃ | abhūtimasamṛddhiṃ ca dvitīyena vināśayet ‖*) **GLOSS**

आद्र्रां पुष्करिणीं पुष्टिं पिंगलां पद्ममालिनीं । चंद्रां हिरण्मयीं लक्ष्मीं जातवेदो म आवह ॥ (*ādrrāṃ puṣkariṇīṃ puṣṭiṃ piṃgalāṃ padmamālinīṃ | caṃdrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

आद्र्रामिति । आद्र्रा० आर्द्रगंधां पुष्करिणीं अभिषेकोद्युक्तदिग्गजशुंडाग्रां पुष्करशब्दो गजशुंडाग्रवाचकः पद्मवतीं वा पद्मलतारूपां वा पुष्टिं पुष्पभिमानिनीं पुष्टिरूपां वा—“पुष्टिरूपेण संस्थितेति” मार्कंडेयवचनात् । पिंगलां पिंगळवर्णां । पद्मालिनीमित्यादि सिद्धमन्यत् ॥ (*ādrrāmiti | ādrrā0 ārdragaṃdhāṃ puṣkariṇīṃ abhiṣekodyuktadiggajaśuṃḍāgrāṃ puṣkaraśabdo gajaśuṃḍāgravācakaḥ padmavatīṃ vā padmalatārūpāṃ vā puṣṭiṃ puṣpabhimāninīṃ puṣṭirūpāṃ vā—“puṣṭirūpeṇa saṃsthiteti” mārkaṃḍeyavacanāt | piṃgalāṃ piṃgaḷavarṇāṃ | padmālinīmityādi siddhamanyat ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

आद्र्रां (*ādrrāṃ*) means she of moist fragrance. पुष्करिणीं (*puṣkariṇīṃ*): she whose elephant-trunk tips, those of the elephants of the quarters, are ready for the anointing (the word पुष्कर (*puṣkara*) denotes the tip of an elephant's trunk); or she who has lotuses, or who has the form of a lotus-creeper. पुष्टिं (*puṣṭiṃ*): the presiding deity of flowers, **NOTE** or she who has the form of nourishment (puṣṭi), according to the statement of Mārkaṇḍeya, पुष्टिरूपेण संस्थितेति (*puṣṭirūpeṇa saṃsthiteti*). पिंगलां (*piṃgalāṃ*): of tawny colour. The rest, beginning with पद्मालिनीमित्यादि (*padmālinīmityādi*), is established.

### Printed p. 323

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**

हेजातवेदः संस्कृताग्ने आद्र्रां शीतलगुणां पुष्करिणीं गजशुंडाग्रवतीं वा पुष्टिं पोषणकरीं सुवर्णां शोभनकांतिं हेममालिनीं हेमपद्मरचितस्रग्विणीं सूर्यामैश्वर्यरूपां हिरण्मयीं स्वर्णरूपां मे मदर्थमावह आवाहनं कुरु ॥ (*hejātavedaḥ saṃskṛtāgne ādrrāṃ śītalaguṇāṃ puṣkariṇīṃ gajaśuṃḍāgravatīṃ vā puṣṭiṃ poṣaṇakarīṃ suvarṇāṃ śobhanakāṃtiṃ hemamālinīṃ hemapadmaracitasragviṇīṃ sūryāmaiśvaryarūpāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ svarṇarūpāṃ me madarthamāvaha āvāhanaṃ kuru ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Jātavedas, O Agni who are consecrated: आद्र्रां (*ādrrāṃ*) she of cool quality; पुष्करिणीं (*puṣkariṇīṃ*) she who has elephant-trunk tips; पुष्टिं (*puṣṭiṃ*) she who brings nourishment; सुवर्णां (*suvarṇāṃ*) she of beautiful radiance; हेममालिनीं (*hemamālinīṃ*) she who wears a garland made of golden lotuses; सूर्यां (*sūryāṃ*) she who has the form of prosperity; हिरण्मयीं (*hiraṇmayīṃ*) she who has the form of gold. For my sake, for my purpose, bring her, perform the invocation.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जातवेदः (*jātavedaḥ*)—O Agni
- आद्र्रां (*ādrrāṃ*)—having the quality of coolness
- पुष्करिणीं (*puṣkariṇīṃ*)—she who is sprinkled with water from the tips of the elephants' trunks for the anointing
- पुष्टिं (*puṣṭiṃ*)—she who has the form of nourishment, or who is the presiding deity of nourishment
- पिंगलां (*piṃgalāṃ*)—she who has a body of a tawny colour (fair, yellow)
- पद्ममालिनीं (*padmamālinīṃ*)—she who wears a garland tied of lotus flowers
- चंद्रां (*caṃdrāṃ*)—she who has a form delightful like the moon
- हिरण्मयीं (*hiraṇmayīṃ*)—she who is endowed with golden ornaments, or who shines with a body made of gold
- लक्ष्मीं (*lakṣmīṃ*)—the great goddess Lakṣmī, or the goddess Śrī
- मे (*me*)—for me (to bring me favour)
- आवह (*āvaha*)—bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The goddess Śrī Lakṣmī, though she shines with exceeding brilliance, is pleasing to the eye, endowed with a cool radiance. For the anointing of this goddess the elephants stand ready in the lake, having taken water in their trunks. She gives nourishment to all beings, has a body shining with a fair colour, wears a garland of lotus flowers, gleams with a golden form, and like the moon is a delight to all. O Agni, bring to me such a Mahālakṣmī, endowed with these qualities.

In this Ṛk and in the next Ṛk the goddess Śrī is praised as being in the form of Gajalakṣmī. In this Ṛk she is described as dwelling within the moon's disc, and in the next Ṛk as dwelling within the sun's disc. The special matters of the fourteenth Ṛk apply also to this thirteenth Ṛk.

#### Ṛk 14

अथ चतुर्दशी युक्ओं अस्य श्रीश्रीसूक्तस्य चतुर्दर्श्यङ्महामंत्रस्य । जातवेदा ऋषिः । श्रीराज्य लक्ष्मीर्देवता । अनुष्टुप् छंदः । श्रीं बीजं । हं शक्तिः । ह्रीं कीलकं । श्रीराज्य लक्ष्मीप्रसादसिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*atha caturdaśī yukoṃ asya śrīśrīsūktasya caturdarśyaṅmahāmaṃtrasya | jātavedā ṛṣiḥ | śrīrājya lakṣmīrdevatā | anuṣṭup chaṃdaḥ | śrīṃ bījaṃ | haṃ śaktiḥ | hrīṃ kīlakaṃ | śrīrājya lakṣmīprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*)

### Printed p. 324

ओं आद्र्रा० पुष्करिणीं यष्टिं—अंगुष्ठाभ्यां वनुः । ओं सुवर्णां—तर्ज नीभ्यां नमः । ओं हेममालिनीं—मध्यमाभ्यां नमः । ओं सूर्यां हिरण्मयीं अनामिका भ्यां नमः । ओं लक्ष्मी—कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । ओं जातवेदो म आ वह करतलकरपृष्ठाभ्यां नमः । ओं आर्दां पुष्करिणीं यष्टिं—हृदयाय नमः । ओं हेममालिनीं—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं सूर्यां हिरण्मयीं—कवचाय हुं । ओं लक्ष्मीं—नेत्रत्रयाय वौषट्। ओं जातवेदो म आवह—अस्त्राय फट् ॥ भूर्भु वस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ ध्यानं ॥ (*oṃ ādrrā0 puṣkariṇīṃ yaṣṭiṃ—aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ vanuḥ | oṃ suvarṇāṃ—tarja nībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ hemamālinīṃ—madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ sūryāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ anāmikā bhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ lakṣmī—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ jātavedo ma ā vaha karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ ārdāṃ puṣkariṇīṃ yaṣṭiṃ—hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ hemamālinīṃ—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ sūryāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ—kavacāya huṃ | oṃ lakṣmīṃ—netratrayāya vauṣaṭ| oṃ jātavedo ma āvaha—astrāya phaṭ ‖ bhūrbhu vassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ ‖*) पद्मं मणिमयं कुंभमिक्षुचापं च बिभ्रतीं । पुष्पबाणं महालक्ष्मीं ध्यायेद्राज्यप्रदायिनीं ॥ एवं ध्यात्वा जपेल्लक्षं राज्यलक्ष्मीं समश्नुते ॥ (*padmaṃ maṇimayaṃ kuṃbhamikṣucāpaṃ ca bibhratīṃ | puṣpabāṇaṃ mahālakṣmīṃ dhyāyedrājyapradāyinīṃ ‖ evaṃ dhyātvā japellakṣaṃ rājyalakṣmīṃ samaśnute ‖*) **GLOSS**

आद्र्रां यःकरिणीं यष्टिं सुवर्णा० हेममालिनीं । सूर्यां हिरण्मयीं लक्ष्मीं जातवेदो म आवह ॥ (*ādrrāṃ yaḥkariṇīṃ yaṣṭiṃ suvarṇā0 hemamālinīṃ | sūryāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

आद्र्रां पूर्वोक्तां यःकरिणीं यष्टिकरां वेत्रहस्तामित्यर्थः । टिलोपश्कांदसः । तद्वतीं दंडकारिणीं दंडकरां वा । यष्टिं दंडरूपां “धर्मदंडस्वरूपिणी” मिति ब्राह्मोक्तेः । सुवर्णां शोभनवर्णां रुक्ष्मरूपिणीं रुक्ष्मिणीं रुक्मदेवतां ना हेममालिनीं हेमविकृतशृंखलादिमालावतीं सूर्यां सूर्य वत्प्रकाशमानां तद्रूपां वा । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥ (*ādrrāṃ pūrvoktāṃ yaḥkariṇīṃ yaṣṭikarāṃ vetrahastāmityarthaḥ | ṭilopaśkāṃdasaḥ | tadvatīṃ daṃḍakāriṇīṃ daṃḍakarāṃ vā | yaṣṭiṃ daṃḍarūpāṃ “dharmadaṃḍasvarūpiṇī” miti brāhmokteḥ | suvarṇāṃ śobhanavarṇāṃ rukṣmarūpiṇīṃ rukṣmiṇīṃ rukmadevatāṃ nā hemamālinīṃ hemavikṛtaśṛṃkhalādimālāvatīṃ sūryāṃ sūrya vatprakāśamānāṃ tadrūpāṃ vā | siddhamanyat ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

आद्र्रां (*ādrrāṃ*) is she who was spoken of before. यःकरिणीं (*yaḥkariṇīṃ*) means she who has a staff (yaṣṭi) in her hand, that is, a cane in her hand; or she who holds a staff, or has a staff in hand. यष्टिं (*yaṣṭiṃ*): of the form of a staff, according to the Brāhma statement धर्मदंडस्वरूपिणी (*dharmadaṃḍasvarūpiṇī*). सुवर्णां (*suvarṇāṃ*): of beautiful colour, who has the form of gold (rukma), the goddess of gold. **NOTE** हेममालिनीं (*hemamālinīṃ*): she who has a garland, chain and the like, fashioned of gold. सूर्यां (*sūryāṃ*): she who shines like the sun, or has his form. The rest is established.

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**

हे अग्ने आद्र्रां पूर्वोक्तां पुष्करिणीं करिहस्ताग्रवतीं यष्टिं पाकशालादिदंडरूपां, इष्टिमितिपाठे यागस्वरूपां पिंगळां कद्रूवर्णां पद्ममालिनीं पूर्वोक्तरूपां चंद्रां संतोषकरीं चंद्रमंडलवासिनीर चंद्ररूपां कर्पूरधारिणीं वा हिरण्मयीं सुवर्णमयीं धनरूपां धनप्रचुरां वा लक्ष्मीं मे मदर्थं ममेति पाठे मत्संबंधिप्रयोजनाय आवह ॥ (*he agne ādrrāṃ pūrvoktāṃ puṣkariṇīṃ karihastāgravatīṃ yaṣṭiṃ pākaśālādidaṃḍarūpāṃ, iṣṭimitipāṭhe yāgasvarūpāṃ piṃgaḷāṃ kadrūvarṇāṃ padmamālinīṃ pūrvoktarūpāṃ caṃdrāṃ saṃtoṣakarīṃ caṃdramaṃḍalavāsinīra caṃdrarūpāṃ karpūradhāriṇīṃ vā hiraṇmayīṃ suvarṇamayīṃ dhanarūpāṃ dhanapracurāṃ vā lakṣmīṃ me madarthaṃ mameti pāṭhe matsaṃbaṃdhiprayojanāya āvaha ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, आद्र्रां (*ādrrāṃ*) is she spoken of before. पुष्करिणीं (*puṣkariṇīṃ*): she who has elephant-trunk tips. यष्टिं (*yaṣṭiṃ*): of the form of a staff such as is used in a kitchen and the like; in the reading इष्टिमितिपाठे (*iṣṭimitipāṭhe*), of the form of a sacrifice. पिंगळां (*piṃgaḷāṃ*): of the colour of kadrū (tawny). पद्ममालिनीं (*padmamālinīṃ*): of the form spoken of before. चंद्रां (*caṃdrāṃ*): she who gives delight, who dwells in the moon's disc, who has the form of the moon, or who bears camphor. हिरण्मयीं (*hiraṇmayīṃ*): made of gold, of the form of wealth, or abounding in wealth. लक्ष्मीं (*lakṣmīṃ*). मे (*me*): for my sake; in the reading ममेति पाठे (*mameti pāṭhe*), for a purpose connected with me; bring her.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जातवेदः (*jātavedaḥ*)—O Agni
- आद्र्रां (*ādrrāṃ*)—of cool nature, or endowed with a cool radiance
- यष्टिं यःकरिणीं (*yaṣṭiṃ yaḥkariṇīṃ*)—she who holds a cane or staff (the staff of dharma) in her hand
- सुवर्णां (*suvarṇāṃ*)—she who has a beautiful colour
- हेममालिनी (*hemamālinī*)—she who wears a garland of flowers made of gold
- सूर्यां (*sūryāṃ*)—she who shines with great brilliance like the sun, or who has the form of the sun

### Printed p. 325

- हिरण्मयिं (*hiraṇmayiṃ*)—she who is adorned with golden ornaments, or who shines with a body made of gold
- लक्ष्मीं (*lakṣmīṃ*)—the goddess called Mahālakṣmī
- मे (*me*)—to my side
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, bring to my side the goddess called Mahālakṣmī, who is of cool nature, or endowed with a cool radiance like the moon; who holds a staff in her hand; who is of beautiful colour; who, wearing a garland of golden flowers, shines with excess of splendour like the sun, or has the form of the sun; and who is adorned with golden ornaments, or has a beautiful golden body.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

श्रीशतकी (*śrīśatakī*) श्रीतत्त्व (*śrītattva*)

ममकारो मदश्चेति वर्तेते पार्श्वयोर्द्वयोः । देवते गजरूपिण्य श्रीदेवीमभिषिंचतः ॥ तयोः शुंडाग्रयोर्भात आशाप्रीत्याह्वयावुभौ । सुवर्णकलश्‌ ताभ्यां तृप्तिपूर्णांभसान्वहं ॥ हृत्पद्मवासिनी देवी चिद्रूपिण्यभिषिच्यते । देहे गृहात्मके विष्टेत्सहानंदेन विष्णुना ॥ एतावान् गजलक्ष्म्यास्तु विभूतीर्विस्तरो ध्रुवः । तां स्मरेदभिषेकाद्र्रा० पुष्टिदां पुष्टिरूपिणीं ॥ (*mamakāro madaśceti vartete pārśvayordvayoḥ | devate gajarūpiṇya śrīdevīmabhiṣiṃcataḥ ‖ tayoḥ śuṃḍāgrayorbhāta āśāprītyāhvayāvubhau | suvarṇakalaś tābhyāṃ tṛptipūrṇāṃbhasānvahaṃ ‖ hṛtpadmavāsinī devī cidrūpiṇyabhiṣicyate | dehe gṛhātmake viṣṭetsahānaṃdena viṣṇunā ‖ etāvān gajalakṣmyāstu vibhūtīrvistaro dhruvaḥ | tāṃ smaredabhiṣekādrrā0 puṣṭidāṃ puṣṭirūpiṇīṃ ‖*)

रुक्माभां स्वप्नधीगम्यां सुवर्णां स्वर्णमालिनीं । सूर्यामैश्वर्यरूपां च सावित्रीं सूर्यरूपिणीं ॥ आत्मसंज्ञां च चिद्रूपां ज्ञानदृष्टिस्वरूपिणीं । चक्षुःप्रकाशबां चैव हिरण्यप्रचुरां तथा ॥ स्वर्णात्मनाविर्भूतां च जातवेदो म आवह । ऋचमेतां जपेन्नित्यं पश्यन्नर्कं दिवा द्विजः ॥ (*rukmābhāṃ svapnadhīgamyāṃ suvarṇāṃ svarṇamālinīṃ | sūryāmaiśvaryarūpāṃ ca sāvitrīṃ sūryarūpiṇīṃ ‖ ātmasaṃjñāṃ ca cidrūpāṃ jñānadṛṣṭisvarūpiṇīṃ | cakṣuḥprakāśabāṃ caiva hiraṇyapracurāṃ tathā ‖ svarṇātmanāvirbhūtāṃ ca jātavedo ma āvaha | ṛcametāṃ japennityaṃ paśyannarkaṃ divā dvijaḥ ‖*)

पद्माकरे तटाके वा प्रत्यहं त्रिसहस्रकं । मंडलत्रयपर्यंतं व्रतदीक्षापरायणः ॥ सुवासिनीशतं पूज्यं सालंकारं सदक्षिणं । (*padmākare taṭāke vā pratyahaṃ trisahasrakaṃ | maṃḍalatrayaparyaṃtaṃ vratadīkṣāparāyaṇaḥ ‖ suvāsinīśataṃ pūjyaṃ sālaṃkāraṃ sadakṣiṇaṃ |*)

### Printed p. 326

सिद्धौ गजांतरैश्वर्यमारोग्यं भाग्यमेव च ॥ सर्वार्थसिद्धिमंते तु सूर्यसायुज्यमाप्नुयात् । मैत्र्या दिवृत्तिभिश्चाद्र्रा० विराजःकरुणां श्रियं ॥ दयाद्रार्रा० वेत्रहस्तां च व्रतदंडस्वरूपिणीं । पिंगळाभां प्रसन्नास्यां पद्ममालाधरां तथा ॥ चंद्रस्थां चंद्ररूपां च चंद्रां चंद्रधरां तथा । हिरण्मयां महालक्ष्मीमृचमेतां जपेन्निशि ॥ (*siddhau gajāṃtaraiśvaryamārogyaṃ bhāgyameva ca ‖ sarvārthasiddhimaṃte tu sūryasāyujyamāpnuyāt | maitryā divṛttibhiścādrrā0 virājaḥkaruṇāṃ śriyaṃ ‖ dayādrārrā0 vetrahastāṃ ca vratadaṃḍasvarūpiṇīṃ | piṃgaḷābhāṃ prasannāsyāṃ padmamālādharāṃ tathā ‖ caṃdrasthāṃ caṃdrarūpāṃ ca caṃdrāṃ caṃdradharāṃ tathā | hiraṇmayāṃ mahālakṣmīmṛcametāṃ japenniśi ‖*)

आरभ्य शुक्लपंचम्यामास्वेव दशरात्रिषु । प्राप्तैकोत्तरवृद्धैव जपेद्वतपरायणः ॥ द्वात्रिंशन्वासपर्यंतं पुरश्चरणमुच्यते । चंद्रमंडलमालक्ष लक्ष्मीं श्रीललितांबिकां ॥ सिद्धौ तु सार्वभौमत्वं वशीकरणमेव च । पूर्णानंदप्रतिष्ठां च कीर्तिभोगं महद्दनं । प्राप्यांते चंद्रसायुज्यं प्राप्नोत्येव न संशयः ॥ (*ārabhya śuklapaṃcamyāmāsveva daśarātriṣu | prāptaikottaravṛddhaiva japedvataparāyaṇaḥ ‖ dvātriṃśanvāsaparyaṃtaṃ puraścaraṇamucyate | caṃdramaṃḍalamālakṣa lakṣmīṃ śrīlalitāṃbikāṃ ‖ siddhau tu sārvabhaumatvaṃ vaśīkaraṇameva ca | pūrṇānaṃdapratiṣṭhāṃ ca kīrtibhogaṃ mahaddanaṃ | prāpyāṃte caṃdrasāyujyaṃ prāpnotyeva na saṃśayaḥ ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

Two deities named Mamakāra and Mada, in the form of elephants, stand on the left and right sides of the goddess Śrī and, having filled with the water of contentment, in their trunks, two golden pots named Hope (āśā) and Love (prīti), anoint every day the goddess Śrī, who dwells in the lotus of the heart, whose nature is consciousness (cit), and who sits on a seat in the house that is the body together with Viṣṇu, whose nature is bliss. This splendour is said to be the particular glory of Gajalakṣmī. This Gajalakṣmī is to be meditated on in the following way: this goddess has a body drenched with the water of the anointing; as the presiding deity of nourishment she gives nourishment to all; she shines with a radiance pure as silver; she appears in dreams; shining with the radiance of gold she wears a garland made of gold; she is radiant like the sun and is the embodiment of prosperity; she has the nature of the Self, the form of consciousness, and the nature of the eye of knowledge; she gives light to the eyes; she has a body radiant with gold, and has come forth from a body of gold. One should meditate thus: O Agni, bring to my side this deity called Gajalakṣmī.

Meditating in this way, standing in water in a lake or a pond and looking at the sun, one should repeat this Ṛk every day three thousand times. Having taken up the vow and initiation for three maṇḍalas (a maṇḍala means forty-eight days) in this manner, and repeated it,

### Printed p. 327

at the end one should honour and gratify a hundred married women (suvāsinī) with clothes, ornaments and gifts. By doing so the mantra is accomplished, and one obtains elephant-wealth and the other kinds of prosperity, health and good fortune, and success in all undertakings, and enjoys happiness. At the end, at the dissolution of the body, he also attains the excellent state called union with the sun (sūryasāyujya).

While repeating this mantra one should meditate on the goddess Śrī as endowed, towards her devotees, with qualities such as affection, tenderness, love and a gaze moist with compassion; as holding a staff in her hand, in the form of the staff of dharma, so that people observe the rules; as having a body of fair radiance and a form delightful like the moon; as bearing the disc of the moon; and as being the form of Mahālakṣmī, resplendent with a body of golden form. One should repeat this Ṛk at night. A man who wishes to perform the preparatory rite (puraścaraṇa) should begin the repetition on the fifth day (pañcamī) of the bright fortnight, and from there for ten days, every night, increasing by one thousand each time, repeat up to the full-moon day. On that day, looking at the disc of the moon, he should imagine that Śrī Mahālakṣmī, famed as Śrī Lalitāmbikā, dwells in that moon-disc. In this way, for twelve months, in each month from the fifth day up to the full-moon day, he should repeat. This way of repeating is named the rule of puraścaraṇa. If the course of repetition done by the rule of puraścaraṇa succeeds, the practiser becomes a universal sovereign. All become subject to him. Complete bliss, fame, the means of enjoyment and abundance of wealth are obtained; having enjoyed all enjoyments in this world for a long time, at the dissolution of the body he attains the great state named union with the moon (candrasāyujya).

Here ends the explanation of the meaning of the fourteenth Ṛk.

#### Ṛk 15

ओं अस्य श्री श्रीसूक्तस्थ ऋक्पंचदशीमहामंत्रस्य । अंगिरा ऋषिः । श्री लक्ष्मी र्देवता ! अनुष्टुप् छंदः । अथवा कुबेर ऋषिः । श्रीमहालक्ष्मीर्देवता ! प्रस्तार पंक्तिशृंदः। ह्रीं बीजं। श्रीं शक्तिः । ह्रीं कीलकं । श्री राज्यसिंहासनस्थमहालक्ष्मीप्रसाद सिद्ध्यर्थे जपे विनियोगः ॥ (*oṃ asya śrī śrīsūktastha ṛkpaṃcadaśīmahāmaṃtrasya | aṃgirā ṛṣiḥ | śrī lakṣmī rdevatā ! anuṣṭup chaṃdaḥ | athavā kubera ṛṣiḥ | śrīmahālakṣmīrdevatā ! prastāra paṃktiśṛṃdaḥ| hrīṃ bījaṃ| śrīṃ śaktiḥ | hrīṃ kīlakaṃ | śrī rājyasiṃhāsanasthamahālakṣmīprasāda siddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ‖*) ओं ह्रीं तां म आवह—अंगुष्ठाभ्यां नमः। ओं श्रीं जातवेदः-तर्जनीभ्यां नमः । ओं ह्रीं लक्ष्मीमनपगामिनीं— मध्यमाभ्यां नमः ! यस्यां हिरण्यं— अनामिकाभ्यां—नमः। ओं श्रीं प्रभूतं गावो दास्योऽश्वान्—कनिष्ठिकाभ्यां नमः । (*oṃ hrīṃ tāṃ ma āvaha—aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ| oṃ śrīṃ jātavedaḥ-tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ lakṣmīmanapagāminīṃ— madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ ! yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ— anāmikābhyāṃ—namaḥ| oṃ śrīṃ prabhūtaṃ gāvo dāsyo 'śvān—kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ |*)

### Printed p. 328

ओं ह्री पुरुषानहं—करतलकरपृष्ठाभ्यां नमः ॥ ओं ह्रीं तां म आवह-हृदयाय नमः । ओं ह्रीं लक्ष्मीमनसगामिनीं—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं श्रीं जातवेदः—शिरसे स्वाहा । ओं ह्रीं लक्ष्मीमनसगामिनीं—शिखाय्कॆ वषट् । ओं ह्रीं यस्यां हिरण्यंकवचाय हुं । ओं श्रीं प्रभूतं गावो दास्योऽश्वान्-नेत्रत्रयाय वौषट् । ओं ह्रीं पुरुषानहं—अस्त्राय फट् ॥ भूर्भुवस्सुवरोमिति दिग्बंधः ॥ ध्यानं ॥ (*oṃ hrī puruṣānahaṃ—karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ ‖ oṃ hrīṃ tāṃ ma āvaha-hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ hrīṃ lakṣmīmanasagāminīṃ—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ śrīṃ jātavedaḥ—śirase svāhā | oṃ hrīṃ lakṣmīmanasagāminīṃ—śikhāykè vaṣaṭ | oṃ hrīṃ yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃkavacāya huṃ | oṃ śrīṃ prabhūtaṃ gāvo dāsyo 'śvān-netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | oṃ hrīṃ puruṣānahaṃ—astrāya phaṭ ‖ bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbaṃdhaḥ ‖ dhyānaṃ ‖*) ध्यायॆल्लक्ष्मीं प्रहसितमुखीं राज्यसिंहासनस्थां मुद्राशक्तिं सकलविनुतां सर्वसंसेव्यमानां । अग्नौ पूज्यामखिलजननीं हेमवर्णां हिरण्यां भाग्योपेतां भुवनसुखदां भार्गवीं भूतधात्रीं ॥ इति ध्यात्वा जपंकुर्यात् ॥ (*dhyāyèllakṣmīṃ prahasitamukhīṃ rājyasiṃhāsanasthāṃ mudrāśaktiṃ sakalavinutāṃ sarvasaṃsevyamānāṃ | agnau pūjyāmakhilajananīṃ hemavarṇāṃ hiraṇyāṃ bhāgyopetāṃ bhuvanasukhadāṃ bhārgavīṃ bhūtadhātrīṃ ‖ iti dhyātvā japaṃkuryāt ‖*) **GLOSS**

तां म आवह जातवेदो लक्ष्मीमनपगामिनीं । यस्यां हिरण्यं प्रभूतं गावो दास्योऽश्वान्विंदेयं पुरुषानहं ॥ (*tāṃ ma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīṃ | yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ prabhūtaṃ gāvo dāsyo 'śvānviṃdeyaṃ puruṣānahaṃ ‖*)

**Bhāṣya of Vidyāraṇya**

इयं ऋक् द्वितीयया ऋचा व्याख्याता । प्रभूतं भूयिष्ठं गावः गाः । दास्यः परिचारिकाः । उभयत्र शसर्थे जस् । इयान् विशेषः .. ॥ (*iyaṃ ṛk dvitīyayā ṛcā vyākhyātā | prabhūtaṃ bhūyiṣṭhaṃ gāvaḥ gāḥ | dāsyaḥ paricārikāḥ | ubhayatra śasarthe jas | iyān viśeṣaḥ .. ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Ṛk has been explained by the second Ṛk. प्रभूतं (*prabhūtaṃ*) means abundant; गावः (*gāvaḥ*) means cows; दास्यः (*dāsyaḥ*) means maidservants. This much is the difference.

**Bhāṣya of Pṛthvīdhara**

हे जातवेदः अग्ने यस्यां लक्ष्म्यामागतायां सत्यां प्रभूतं हिरण्यं पुष्कलं धनं गावः गोमहिष्यादीन् दासीजनान् अश्वान् तुरगादीन् पुरुषान् पुत्रमित्रकलत्रादीन् विंदेयमहं लभेयं । अनपगामिनीं निवृत्तिरहितां तां तादृशीं लक्ष्मीं श्रियं मे मदर्थं आवह आवाहनं कुरु ॥ (*he jātavedaḥ agne yasyāṃ lakṣmyāmāgatāyāṃ satyāṃ prabhūtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ puṣkalaṃ dhanaṃ gāvaḥ gomahiṣyādīn dāsījanān aśvān turagādīn puruṣān putramitrakalatrādīn viṃdeyamahaṃ labheyaṃ | anapagāminīṃ nivṛttirahitāṃ tāṃ tādṛśīṃ lakṣmīṃ śriyaṃ me madarthaṃ āvaha āvāhanaṃ kuru ‖*)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Jātavedas, O Agni: when that Lakṣmī has come, in whom there is प्रभूतं हिरण्यं (*prabhūtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ*), abundant gold, that is, plentiful wealth, I would obtain cows, that is, buffaloes and the like, servant-women, horses, that is, steeds and the like, and पुरुषान् (*puruṣān*), that is, sons, friends, wives and the like. अनपगामिनीं (*anapagāminīṃ*) means she who does not turn away. Bring, perform the invocation of, such a Lakṣmī, Śrī, for my sake, for my purpose.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जातवेदः (*jātavedaḥ*)—O Agni
- अनपगामिनीं (*anapagāminīṃ*)—she who never leaves me, that is, who dwells firmly in my house
- तां (*tāṃ*)—that renowned one
- लक्ष्मीं (*lakṣmīṃ*)—the goddess Śrī, renowned as Mahālakṣmī

### Printed p. 329

- मे (*me*)—to my side, or to show me favour
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—bring
- यस्यां (*yasyāṃ*)—the goddess Śrī, by whose coming to me
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- प्रभूतं (*prabhūtaṃ*)—abundant
- हिरण्यं (*hiraṇyaṃ*)—gold, or wealth such as money and gold
- गावः (*gāvaḥ*)—cows (wealth of cattle)
- दास्यः (*dāsyaḥ*)—male and female servants, that is, attendants
- अश्वान् (*aśvān*)—horses
- पुरुषान् (*puruṣān*)—people such as sons, friends and wives
- विंदेयं (*viṃdeyaṃ*)—may I obtain (by the grace of the goddess); bring such a goddess Śrī to my side

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, bring to my side that goddess Śrī, by whose coming to my house I shall obtain, through the favour of that goddess, abundant wealth such as money and gold, wealth of cattle such as cows, male and female servants, horses, and the circle of relatives such as sons, friends and wives.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

मंत्रकल्पार्णवे (*maṃtrakalpārṇave*)

पंचदश्या ऋचः कुबेरऋषिः प्रस्तारपंक्तिश्छंदः । ह्रीं श्रीं ह्रीमिति बीजानि द्विधाविभक्तमंत्रेण सबीजत्रयेण पल्लवेन त्रिवारमनुलोमविलोमपरिवर्तनतः षोढा न्यासाः । (*paṃcadaśyā ṛcaḥ kuberaṛṣiḥ prastārapaṃktiśchaṃdaḥ | hrīṃ śrīṃ hrīmiti bījāni dvidhāvibhaktamaṃtreṇa sabījatrayeṇa pallavena trivāramanulomavilomaparivartanataḥ ṣoḍhā nyāsāḥ |*) ध्यायेल्लक्ष्मीं प्रहसितमुखीं राज्यसिंहासनस्थां मुद्राशक्तिं सकलविनुतां सर्वसंसेव्यमानां । अग्नौ पूज्यामखिलजननीं हेमवर्णां हिरण्यां भाग्योपेतां भुवनसुखदां भार्गवीं भूतिधात्रीं ॥ (*dhyāyellakṣmīṃ prahasitamukhīṃ rājyasiṃhāsanasthāṃ mudrāśaktiṃ sakalavinutāṃ sarvasaṃsevyamānāṃ | agnau pūjyāmakhilajananīṃ hemavarṇāṃ hiraṇyāṃ bhāgyopetāṃ bhuvanasukhadāṃ bhārgavīṃ bhūtidhātrīṃ ‖*)

मंत्रोऽयं धान्यराशेरुपरि पद्माक्षमालया तुलसीकाष्ठमालया ना प्रत्यहमष्टोत्तरत्रिसहस्रप्रकारेण सहस्रदिवसजपात्सिद्धो भवति । व्याघ्राजिने महेंद्रनीलाक्षमालया पंचलक्षजपाच्छत्रून् जयति । वस्त्रासने दर्भास्तरणे वा रुद्राक्षमालया नवलक्षजपाद्विद्यां प्राप्नोति । वेत्रासने सुवर्णाक्षमालया कोटिजपाद्राज्यं प्राप्नुयात् । ऐहिकसर्वकामप्रदोऽयं मंत्रः । अर्थतस्त्वेषा द्वितीयामृचमनुकरोति । अन्यदन्यत्रोक्तमेवेति समानं ॥ (*maṃtro 'yaṃ dhānyarāśerupari padmākṣamālayā tulasīkāṣṭhamālayā nā pratyahamaṣṭottaratrisahasraprakāreṇa sahasradivasajapātsiddho bhavati | vyāghrājine maheṃdranīlākṣamālayā paṃcalakṣajapācchatrūn jayati | vastrāsane darbhāstaraṇe vā rudrākṣamālayā navalakṣajapādvidyāṃ prāpnoti | vetrāsane suvarṇākṣamālayā koṭijapādrājyaṃ prāpnuyāt | aihikasarvakāmaprado 'yaṃ maṃtraḥ | arthatastveṣā dvitīyāmṛcamanukaroti | anyadanyatroktameveti samānaṃ ‖*)

इति पंचदशी ऋग्विवृता (*iti paṃcadaśī ṛgvivṛtā*)

इति श्रीसूक्तभाष्यं (*iti śrīsūktabhāṣyaṃ*)

### Printed p. 330

##### Anuvāda

For this Ṛk of the Śrīsūkta, the fifteenth Ṛk, which begins तां म (*tāṃ ma*), Kubera is the seer; Lakṣmī is the deity; the metre is Prastārapaṅkti. For the convenience of nyāsa the mantra is to be divided into two parts, the three seed syllables are to be joined in direct and reverse order, and the sixfold nyāsa is to be performed. The meditation verse is as follows.

ध्यायॆल्लक्ष्मीं प्रहसितमुखीं राज्यसिंहासनस्थां मुद्राशक्तिं सकलविनुतां सर्वसंसेव्यमानां अग्नौ पूज्यामखिलजननीं हेमवर्णां हिरण्यां भाग्योपेतां भुवनसुखदां भागवीं भूतिधात्रीं ॥ (*dhyāyèllakṣmīṃ prahasitamukhīṃ rājyasiṃhāsanasthāṃ mudrāśaktiṃ sakalavinutāṃ sarvasaṃsevyamānāṃ agnau pūjyāmakhilajananīṃ hemavarṇāṃ hiraṇyāṃ bhāgyopetāṃ bhuvanasukhadāṃ bhāgavīṃ bhūtidhātrīṃ ‖*)

This goddess Śrī, bearing the name Lakṣmī, shines seated on the royal throne, with a gentle smile on her face, holding in her hands the gestures of granting boons, giving fearlessness and the like. She is praised by all people. Invoked in the fire, she is worthy of worship. She is the mother of the whole world. She shines with the radiance of a golden-hued body. The body of this goddess is wholly of gold. Being the mistress of fortune, she brings happiness to the people of all worlds. Born in the family of Bhṛgu and bearing prosperity and the other glories, this goddess Śrī is to be meditated on in this way: such is the main purport.

The practiser who repeats the mantra should sit upon a heap of grain, take as his rosary either a lotus-seed rosary or a rosary of tulasī wood, and repeat this Ṛk every day three thousand one hundred and eight times, for a thousand days; for such men the mantra is accomplished. If he spreads for a seat a tiger skin, sits upon it, and repeats it five hundred thousand times with a rosary of sapphire (indranīla) beads, he conquers his enemies in battles and the like and wins victory over all quarters. If he spreads a cloth, or sits on a seat of darbha grass, and repeats it nine hundred thousand times with a rudrākṣa rosary, he obtains all knowledge. If he sits on a seat of cane (vetra), that is, a seat made of rattan, and repeats it ten million times with a rosary studded with golden beads, he obtains a kingdom. This mantra grants all desires that are enjoyed in this world (aihika). In meaning this mantra is just like the second Ṛk of this sūkta.

Here ends the explanation of the meaning of the fifteenth Ṛk.

Here the Śrīsūkta ends.

### Printed p. 331

#### Ṛk 16

निरुक्तकारोक्तफलस्तुतिः (*niruktakāroktaphalastutiḥ*)

यः शुचिः प्रयतो भूत्वा जुहुयादाज्यमन्वहं । श्रियः पंचदशर्चं च श्रीकामः सततं जपेत् ॥ १६ ॥ (*yaḥ śuciḥ prayato bhūtvā juhuyādājyamanvahaṃ | śriyaḥ paṃcadaśarcaṃ ca śrīkāmaḥ satataṃ japet ‖ 16 ‖*)

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the man who
- श्रीकामः (*śrīkāmaḥ*)—desires wealth; that man
- प्रयतः (*prayataḥ*)—with effort
- शुचिर्भूत्वा (*śucirbhūtvā*)—having become pure by bathing and the like
- श्रियः (*śriyaḥ*)—of this Śrīsūkta that treats of the goddess Śrī
- पंचदशर्चं (*paṃcadaśarcaṃ*)—with the fifteen Ṛks
- अन्वहं (*anvahaṃ*)—every day
- आज्यं (*ājyaṃ*)—with the offering of ghee
- जुहयात् (*juhayāt*)—must offer in the consecrated fire
- च (*ca*)—and the fifteen Ṛks of this sūkta
- सततं (*satataṃ*)—always, that is, every day
- जपेत् (*japet*)—must repeat

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The man who desires to obtain the favour of the goddess Śrī and wealth should, with effort, purified by bathing and the like, offer every day in the consecrated fire oblations of ghee with the fifteen Ṛks of this Śrīsūkta. And he should also repeat this sūkta every day according to rule.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

निरुक्तकारोक्तफलस्तुतिः (*niruktakāroktaphalastutiḥ*)

ओं । यः शुचिः प्रयतो भूत्वा जुहुयादाज्यमन्वहं । श्रियः पंचदशर्चं च श्रीकामः सततं जपेत् ॥ १६ ॥ (*oṃ | yaḥ śuciḥ prayato bhūtvā juhuyādājyamanvahaṃ | śriyaḥ paṃcadaśarcaṃ ca śrīkāmaḥ satataṃ japet ‖ 16 ‖*)

यः श्रीकामः स शुचिः प्रयतश्च भूत्वा सततं संकल्पदिनादि पंचदशाब्देषु प्रत्यहं स्वशाखोक्तविधानेनाग्निं संस्कृत्य श्रीसूक्तपंचदशऋग्भिः पंचदशाज्याहुतीर्हुत्वा ततः परं श्रियः श्रीसूक्तपंचदशर्चं साकल्येनैकवारमिति यथाशक्त्यखंडपारायणं कुर्यात् । अथवा पंचदशवारमखंडपारायणं कृत्वा तत एकैकामृचं विधिवद्यथासंख्यं जपेत् । तदशक्तौ लौकिकेऽग्नावपि पंचदश ऋग्भिः प्रत्यहं पंचदशाज्याहुतीर्हुत्वा पंचदश वारं श्रीसूक्तं जपेत्तस्य श्रीकामस्य तस्मिन्नेव (*yaḥ śrīkāmaḥ sa śuciḥ prayataśca bhūtvā satataṃ saṃkalpadinādi paṃcadaśābdeṣu pratyahaṃ svaśākhoktavidhānenāgniṃ saṃskṛtya śrīsūktapaṃcadaśaṛgbhiḥ paṃcadaśājyāhutīrhutvā tataḥ paraṃ śriyaḥ śrīsūktapaṃcadaśarcaṃ sākalyenaikavāramiti yathāśaktyakhaṃḍapārāyaṇaṃ kuryāt | athavā paṃcadaśavāramakhaṃḍapārāyaṇaṃ kṛtvā tata ekaikāmṛcaṃ vidhivadyathāsaṃkhyaṃ japet | tadaśaktau laukike 'gnāvapi paṃcadaśa ṛgbhiḥ pratyahaṃ paṃcadaśājyāhutīrhutvā paṃcadaśa vāraṃ śrīsūktaṃ japettasya śrīkāmasya tasminneva*)

### Printed p. 332

जन्मनि महती श्रीप्राप्तिर्भविष्यतीति विज्ञायते । विधिवदनुष्ठानासमर्थस्य तु जन्मांतरे फलानुभवो भविष्यतीति सिद्धांतः ॥ (*janmani mahatī śrīprāptirbhaviṣyatīti vijñāyate | vidhivadanuṣṭhānāsamarthasya tu janmāṃtare phalānubhavo bhaviṣyatīti siddhāṃtaḥ ‖*) तदुक्तं मंत्ररहस्ये (*taduktaṃ maṃtrarahasye*)

अधिकारी यथादेशे यथाकाले यथामनुं । यादृग्विधानसंधानः प्रजपेत्तादृशं फलं ॥ कालदेशक्रियारूपाद्यनुरूपं लभेन्नरः । अत्युत्कटेन पुण्येन तस्मिन्नेव हि जन्मनि ॥ तत्फलं प्राप्नुयात्सिद्दो नात्र कार्या विचारणा । पुण्यस्यानुत्कटत्वे तु चिरात्किंचित्सलं लभेत् ॥ प्रतिबंधकबाहुल्ये फलं जन्मांतरे भवेत् । न देवतोषणं व्यर्थं भविष्यति कदाचन ॥ तस्मान्मंत्रं जपेद्योगी यतः शुद्धेंद्रियक्रियः ॥ इति ॥ (*adhikārī yathādeśe yathākāle yathāmanuṃ | yādṛgvidhānasaṃdhānaḥ prajapettādṛśaṃ phalaṃ ‖ kāladeśakriyārūpādyanurūpaṃ labhennaraḥ | atyutkaṭena puṇyena tasminneva hi janmani ‖ tatphalaṃ prāpnuyātsiddo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | puṇyasyānutkaṭatve tu cirātkiṃcitsalaṃ labhet ‖ pratibaṃdhakabāhulye phalaṃ janmāṃtare bhavet | na devatoṣaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ bhaviṣyati kadācana ‖ tasmānmaṃtraṃ japedyogī yataḥ śuddheṃdriyakriyaḥ ‖ iti ‖*)

##### Anuvāda

The man who desires wealth, grain and the like, having become pure, from the day on which he makes his resolve, for fifteen years, should offer in the consecrated fire fifteen oblations of ghee, one with each Ṛk of this Śrīsūkta, and every day repeat those fifteen Ṛks according to rule. Or else he should perform the recitation as far as he is able. Or, putting the fifteen Ṛks together, he should perform the unbroken recitation (akhaṇḍa-pārāyaṇa) and afterwards repeat each single Ṛk the prescribed number of times. If he lacks the strength even for that, he should every day offer fifteen oblations of ghee with the fifteen Ṛks in the ordinary fire itself and afterwards repeat the Śrīsūkta fifteen times. By this it is known by experience that the man who desires wealth obtains great prosperity in this very birth. If one lacks the strength to perform the observance according to rule, without the slightest deficiency, then even if he does the repetition and the rest as far as he is able, the fruit comes to him accordingly without fail. If, while the repetition and the rest are being done, many obstacles arise and the observance is not completed, the experience of the fruit will come in a later birth: this is the established conclusion. This matter is stated in the work called Mantrarahasya in the following way.

The man who is qualified to repeat this sūkta, even if for other reasons he is unable to perform the repetition according to rule without the slightest deficiency, receives the fruit in as far as he repeats this mantra, suitably to place and time, that is, as the occasion allows. By the power of exceedingly great merit the mantra is accomplished in this very birth, and its fruit too is obtained. In this matter there is no doubt. If the power of merit is not so great, some fruit is obtained a little, after some time.

### Printed p. 333

If many obstacles arise and the repetition is not carried on, then however small the repetition performed up to then, its fruit will be obtained in the next birth. Repetition, worship and the like done for the pleasure of the deity are never in vain. Therefore the yogin (or the one who repeats) should, having conquered his senses and being pure, repeat these mantras of the Śrīsūkta with devotion.

Here ends the explanation of the Ṛk that is the praise of the fruit.

Notice: in the Śrīsūkta that is stated among the Appendix mantras of the Ṛgveda there are only fifteen Ṛks. One Ṛk, which describes the praise of the fruit, stands at its end. It is well known as the mantra in the form of the praise of the fruit of the Śrīsūkta, spoken by the author of the Nirukta. After reciting these, it is the custom to recite some verses that praise Lakṣmī, or the goddess Śrī. Since these are in easy language, no one has written commentaries and the like upon them. In their number and in the order of recitation, differences and variant readings are seen, as they are in use in different places. As far as possible, these verses and their main purport are explained here in the order that is customary in reciting them.

पद्मासने पद्मऊरू पद्माक्षी पद्मसंभवे । त्वं मां भजस्व पद्माक्षी येन सौख्यं लभाम्यहं ॥ (*padmāsane padmaūrū padmākṣī padmasaṃbhave | tvaṃ māṃ bhajasva padmākṣī yena saukhyaṃ labhāmyahaṃ ‖*)

O Mahālakṣmī, you have made the lotus itself your seat and sit upon it. Your thighs are tender like the lotus. Your eyes are wide and charming like the lotus. You are born in the lotus itself. O Lakṣmī, whose eyes are charming like the lotus, show favour to me, come to my side and abide in my dwelling-place. Thereby I shall come to obtain happiness.

अश्वदायिा गोदायिा धनदायिा महाधने । धनं मे जुषतां देवि सर्वकामार्थसिद्धये ॥ (*aśvadāyiā godāyiā dhanadāyiā mahādhane | dhanaṃ me juṣatāṃ devi sarvakāmārthasiddhaye ‖*)

O Lakṣmī who have great wealth, you are the giver to devotees of horses, cows and riches. Therefore, O goddess, for the accomplishment of all desired aims, that is, so that all the wishes that I desire may be fulfilled, bestow wealth upon me and show favour.

पुत्रपौत्रधनं धान्यं हस्यश्वादिगवे रथं । प्रजानां भवसि माता आयुष्मंतं करोतु मां ॥ (*putrapautradhanaṃ dhānyaṃ hasyaśvādigave rathaṃ | prajānāṃ bhavasi mātā āyuṣmaṃtaṃ karotu māṃ ‖*)
